*Andreas Schwung,Steve Yuwono,Sofiene Lassoued,Dorothea Schwung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模型的强化学习框架，在策略训练中引入近似逆过程模型，将驱动动力学与状态空间动力学解耦，使RL训练仅在任务空间进行，并在模块化生产测试台上验证了性能与训练速度的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度柔性、模块化的制造系统难以用传统方法实现数据驱动的自学习控制；直接将强化学习应用于此类系统面临驱动动力学与状态空间动力学耦合导致的学习效率低、训练慢的问题。

Method: 采用一种新的基于模型的强化学习框架，在强化策略训练中引入近似逆过程模型，从而解耦驱动动力学与状态空间动力学的学习，使基于RL的训练完全在任务空间内进行；并提出一种轻量级前馈架构用于近似逆模型，将其集成到标准RL算法的策略网络中。

Result: 在包含异构生产模块的实验室模块化生产测试台上应用该方法，结果表明模块化制造单元在性能与训练速度两方面均获得效率提升，尤其在离策略（off-policy）算法上表现更为显著。

Conclusion: 该基于模型、引入近似逆过程模型的强化学习框架可有效提升模块化制造系统自学习控制的性能与训练效率，为高度柔性模块化制造的数据驱动控制提供了可行途径。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach for data-driven self-learning control of highly flexible, modular manufacturing systems. Specifically, we employ a novel framework for model-based reinforcement learning which introduces approximate inverse process models within the training of reinforcement policies. This approach disentangles the learning of actuation dynamics and the dynamics in state space, resulting in RL-based training solely within the task space. We propose a lightweight feedforward architecture for approximate inverse models and integrate them within the policy network of standard RL algorithms. We apply the approach to a laboratory modular production testbed with heterogeneous production modules. The results underline the efficiency improvements for modular manufacturing units in terms of both performance and training speed, particularly for off-policy algorithms.

</details>


### [65] [MAPLE: Memory-Augmented Planning with Language and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11636)
*Kesheng Chen,Yamin Hu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAPLE，一种记忆增强的语言与进化优化智能体，用于在连续自然语言请求下维护并演化优化问题，并构建 NLDO 基准进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域从业者了解业务约束但缺乏运筹优化专业知识或专门支持；LLM 优化智能体虽可将自然语言需求转化为模型或求解程序，但真实运营是动态的，需求、资源和优先级不断变化，需要更新数据、约束和目标。以孤立请求为中心的方法难以支持快速适应，也无法保留已有决策和复用有用的搜索结果。

Method: MAPLE 结合基于语言的问题构建、数学规划与进化搜索，并保留优化程序、已接受方案、历史更新与候选解以服务后续请求。作者同时提出 NLDO 基准，包含 15 条轨迹和 180 次更新，覆盖选择、排程、排班、路径规划和云资源放置等任务。

Result: 在主评测中，MAPLE 完成全部轨迹，在线标量质量为 0.951，Pareto 超体积比为 0.875。受控对比进一步表明，维护可执行状态可提升更新有效性，并能在大幅修改中保留有用的搜索信息。

Conclusion: MAPLE 通过记忆增强与语言—进化协同，支持动态优化问题的持续维护，在动态运营场景中表现出较强的适应能力与解质量保持能力。

Abstract: Domain practitioners understand their business constraints but may lack operations-research expertise or dedicated support. LLM-based optimization agents translate natural-language requirements into models or solver programs that established optimization tools can execute. This progress makes optimization more accessible, but real-world operations are dynamic: changing demand, resources, and priorities require updates to data, constraints, and objectives. Methods centered on isolated requests offer limited support for rapid adaptation that preserves earlier decisions and reuses useful search results. We introduce MAPLE (Memory-Augmented Planning with Language and Evolution), an agent for maintaining optimization problems through successive natural-language requests. MAPLE combines language-based problem construction with mathematical programming and evolutionary search. It retains the optimization program, accepted plans, earlier updates, and candidate solutions for subsequent requests. We introduce NLDO, a benchmark of 15 trajectories and 180 updates spanning selection, scheduling, rostering, routing, and cloud-resource placement. In the main evaluation, MAPLE completes all trajectories and achieves online scalar quality of 0.951 and a Pareto hypervolume ratio of 0.875. Controlled comparisons further show that maintaining executable state improves update validity and can preserve useful search information across substantial revisions.

</details>


### [66] [Autonomy, Social Norms, and Alignment: Towards a Developmental Framework for Autonomous Artificial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11660)
*Marica Notte,Ludovica Marinucci,Vieri Giuliano Santucci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该摘要提出：具身智能体在动态未知环境中难以仅靠预训练数据和人类反馈实现适应，需要内在动机等高层机制驱动自主探索；而自主性提升又使价值对齐更加困难。作者主张对齐应是一个基于经验的认识论过程，通过情境化原则逐步构建复杂规范，并将"监管沙箱"视为AI的教学环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型模型虽具备泛化与复杂输出能力，但其向具身智能体迁移时受限于对既有数据集与人类反馈策略的依赖，在动态或未知情境中不足；智能体必须通过与环境直接交互获取知识。同时，引入内在动机等高层机制会提升自主性，使确保智能体与人类目标对齐变得更为复杂，尤其在非结构化与动态环境中，预设规则不足以应对。因此需要探讨如何在保持适应性的同时实现对齐。

Method: 本文属理论/立场性论述，非实验方法。其论证路径为：首先指出具身智能中自主探索与对齐之间的张力；继而借用认识论视角，主张规范须"植根于经验"，即从简单、情境化的原则出发，经由经验、自主学习和与其他道德主体的合作，逐步构建更复杂的规则；并以儿童通过探索环境和参与集体实践来学习社会规范作类比，提出人工智能体也需要"被教育"以实现对齐；最后援引Dennett的观点，认为道德主体地位并非先天赋予，而是依据负责任地管理日益增加的自由度之能力逐步被归赋。

Result: 该文未报告实验数据或量化结果，其"结果"为概念性结论：其一，对齐不应被理解为对预设规则的静态遵循，而应被视为在非结构化、动态情境中逐步生成的过程；其二，将"监管沙箱"重新定义为AI的教学/教育环境，即动态空间，在其中对齐作为形成性过程发展，通过在复杂度递增的场景中的交互与合作，渐进地塑造自主行为。

Conclusion: 对齐是一种渐进的教育与认识论过程：规范由情境化经验中生长而来，道德主体地位随自由度管理能力的提升而被逐步归赋；监管沙箱可作为实现这一过程的 pedagogical 环境，在复杂度递增的交互与合作中塑造智能体的自主行为。

Abstract: In recent years, artificial intelligence has made extraordinary progress thanks to large-scale models capable of generalization and the generation of complex outputs. However, transferring this potential into embodied agents reveals a significant limitation: the most advanced systems rely on pre-existing datasets and human feedback strategies that are powerful but insufficient in dynamic or unknown contexts. To adapt, an agent must acquire knowledge through direct interaction with its environment. One strategy to address this challenge involves introducing higher-level mechanisms, such as intrinsic motivations, which leverage curiosity and competence, to guide exploration and learning in complex environments. While this flexibility expands autonomy, it complicates the task of ensuring agents remain aligned with human goals. Alignment, already a challenge for artificial systems in general, becomes even more complex in unstructured and dynamic contexts where predefined rules prove insufficient. To be effective and adaptable, norms must be rooted in experience through an epistemological process that starting from simple, situated principles allows for the gradual construction of more complex rules through experience, autonomous learning, and cooperation with other moral agents. Similarly to children learning social norms by exploring their environment and participating in collective practices, artificial agents must also be educated toward alignment. Following Dennett, the status of a moral agent is not innate but is attributed gradually based on the ability to responsibly manage increasing degrees of freedom. From this perspective, the regulatory sandboxes can be viewed as pedagogical environments for AI: dynamic spaces where alignment develops as a formative process, progressively shaping autonomous behaviors through interaction and cooperation in scenarios of increasing complexity.

</details>


### [67] [Geospatial AI, Dataverse Metadata, and the Study of Place-Based Government](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11674)
*Danny EBanks,Devika Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对Harvard Dataverse的15万余个数据集构建知识图谱（10.27万数据集、21.6万节点、52.8万边），实现地理元数据的结构化组织与政策相关性检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harvard Dataverse拥有超15万个研究数据集，但其地理信息由存缴者以自由文本录入，从未被整合为可检索的结构，导致地理元数据难以复用与发现。

Method: 基于仓储公开数据与元数据构建知识图谱：将102,650个数据集置于包含215,985个节点、528,003条边的网络中，连接数据集与关键词、出版物、学科、期刊及地点；统计地理字段覆盖与连通性；用保守关键词检索识别政策相关数据集；并通过五个数据集案例与扩展用例展示应用。

Result: 43,991个数据集（42.9%）至少含一个地理空间字段、地理覆盖、地理单元或边界框；96.9%的节点位于单一连通分量；关键词检索识别出7,654个地理标注数据集（17.4%）具有直接政策相关性，以选举与立法机构为最大聚类，其后为政府行政、卫生政策、交通与教育。

Conclusion: 核心障碍是地点消解——同一地点表现为多个互不连通的节点。该图谱为AI驱动的元数据增强与实体消解提供了具体场景，并揭示了数据覆盖向美国、城市级数据倾斜的问题。

Abstract: Harvard Dataverse hosts over 150,000 research datasets, but the geographic information those datasets carry is entered as free text by depositors and has never been assembled into a searchable structure. We construct a knowledge graph from the repository's public data and metadata, organizing 102,650 datasets within a 215,985-node network of 528,003 edges linking datasets to keywords, publications, subjects, journals, and locations. Of those datasets, 43,991 (42.9 percent) carry at least one geospatial field, geographic coverage, geographic unit, or a bounding box and 96.9 percent of all nodes sit in a single connected component, so datasets remain reachable from one another even when their geospatial metadata share nothing in common. A conservative keyword search identifies 7,654 geospatially tagged datasets (17.4 percent) as directly policy-relevant, with elections and legislatures the largest cluster, followed by government administration, health policy, transportation, and education. Five datasets illustrate how this metadata behaves across policy domains and spatial scales, and an extended use case shows how community language models, stance detection with geographic aggregation, and partisan language bridging tools can attach discourse to place. The central obstacle is place resolution: the same location appears as many disconnected nodes. We argue that the graph provides a concrete setting for developing AI-driven metadata enrichment and entity resolution, and we document its coverage skew toward American, city-level data.

</details>


### [68] [COBRA-Skills: Contextual Bandit-Guided Evolution for Agent Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11682)
*Pingchen Lu,Xiangyi Wang,Xiang Li,Jie Mao,Zikun Qu,Junfeng Luo,Yao Shu,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出COBRA-Skills框架，将技能优化建模为动态演化候选空间上的预算约束序贯优化，结合上下文赌博机优先排序与证据驱动的技能演化，在六个基准上取得最优平均性能并将优化成本降低55–58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的技能优化方法依赖高成本执行评估和大量任务数据，效率低下，因此需要一种在有限预算和小样本条件下仍能高效优化的技能获取方法。

Method: 将技能优化形式化为动态演化候选空间上的预算化序贯优化问题，耦合上下文赌博机引导的候选优先排序与基于执行反馈的证据驱动技能演化，选择性地把评估预算分配给有潜力或信息量大的候选，并持续精炼技能种群。

Result: 在六个异构智能体基准和三个目标模型上，COBRA-Skills的平均性能在所有对比方法中最优；相比SkillOpt优化成本降低55–58%；每个基准仅需50个独特优化样本；对智能体harness变化保持稳健，且在目标模型自身用于技能生成与精炼时依然有效。

Conclusion: COBRA-Skills证明了通过预算化序贯优化与赌博机引导的候选选择，可在极少样本下高效蒸馏可复用技能，兼具性能优势与成本效率，并具备良好的鲁棒性与自举能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can benefit from reusable skills distilled from prior task experience, yet existing skill optimization methods often rely on costly execution-based evaluation and substantial task data. We introduce \textbf{COBRA-Skills}, an efficient framework that formulates skill optimization as budgeted sequential optimization over a dynamically evolving candidate space. COBRA-Skills couples contextual-bandit-guided prioritization with evidence-grounded skill evolution, selectively allocating evaluations to promising or informative candidates while continually refining the skill population from execution feedback. Across six heterogeneous agent benchmarks and three target models, COBRA-Skills consistently achieves the strongest average performance among compared methods, while reducing optimization cost by 55--58\% relative to SkillOpt and using only 50 unique optimization examples per benchmark. Further analyses show that COBRA-Skills remains robust to changes in the agent harness and performs effectively when the target model itself is used for skill generation and refinement.

</details>


### [69] [When Agents Disagree: Bayesian Backward Reasoning as a Label-Free Anchor for Multi-Agent Collective Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11709)
*Ken Chen,Wei Wang,Sachith Seneviratne,Hansani Weeratunge,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过贝叶斯反向推理构建反向后验，利用前向与反向两种不同因式分解估计之间的错误不相关性，以Jensen-Shannon散度衡量跨路径一致性，并据此设计硬选择、软重加权与对数线性融合三种聚合策略，在DDXPlus上验证其优于现有集体决策方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个LLM智能体给出冲突答案时，如何决策决定了智能体多样性是提升性能还是放大共同错误。现有的集体决策方法（投票、选举规则、LLM评判）均依赖前向推理，即单向地将证据映射为标签，因此其聚合的估计共享同一"证据-标签"因式分解，容易继承前向池中相关性的错误。

Method: 针对每个实例，通过显式似然函数进行贝叶斯反向推理，构建反向后验。前向与反向后验构成对真实后验的两种不同因式分解近似，因其错误不易重合，故用Jensen-Shannon散度对智能体按跨路径一致性排序。基于该信号提出三种策略：硬选择（MinJS）、软重加权（FwdJS）与对数线性融合（LogLin）。此外，在有标注数据时可进行轻量两阶段校准以精炼反向锚点。

Result: 在DDXPlus上对五种LLM骨干进行评估：MinJS在所有骨干上均优于随机选择；FwdJS总体优于最强基线；LogLin在评估方法中表现最佳，且在智能体意见分歧的子集上增益最大。反向后验虽单独准确率较弱，但作为锚点比纯前向方案更有用，为集体决策提供互补信息。

Conclusion: 前向与反向两种因式分解的错误不相关性是可利用的有效信号；反向后验作为互补锚点能提升集体决策性能，且在有标注数据时可通过两阶段校准进一步优化。

Abstract: When multiple LLM agents yield conflicting answers, the decision-making process dictates whether agent diversity improves performance or merely compounds shared errors. Existing collective decision-making methods, including voting, electoral rules, and LLM judges, rely on forward reasoning: they map evidence to labels in one direction. Although these methods can combine diverse forward traces, they still aggregate estimates that share this evidence-to-label factorization and can inherit correlated errors within the forward pool. We therefore construct a reverse posterior for each instance through Bayesian backward reasoning from an explicit likelihood. The forward and reverse posteriors provide differently factorized approximations of the underlying posterior. Because estimates from different factorizations may tend to share the same error less often, we use Jensen-Shannon divergence to rank agents by cross-path consistency. This cross-path consistency signal underlies three strategies: hard selection (MinJS), soft reweighting (FwdJS), and log-linear fusion (LogLin). Evaluated on DDXPlus across five LLM backbones, our proposed strategies show consistent improvements: MinJS outperforms random selection across all backbones, FwdJS generally improves over the strongest baseline, and LogLin achieves the best performance among the evaluated methods, with its largest gains on the subset where the agents disagree. Despite its weaker standalone accuracy, the reverse posterior serves as a more useful anchor than forward-only alternatives, providing complementary information for collective decision-making. When labeled data are available, a lightweight two-stage calibration can further refine the reverse anchor and improve aggregation performance.

</details>


### [70] [SIRF: A Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model for Industrial Content Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11752)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIRF 通过继续预训练将平台复杂风控策略内化进模型权重，在高精度、秒级延迟的纯判别式部署下显著提升风险自动处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业内容风控的真实部署约束不是平均准确率，而是在高精度与秒级延迟下能自动处理多少风险；策略以显式规则形式部署时难以兼顾精度与延迟。

Method: 提出 SIRF（Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model）：借助 EntiGraph、MAGA 改写与账号级思维链（CoT）在无额外人工标注下合成策略数据，并通过继续预训练（CPT）将策略内化到权重中，实现超低延迟的 verdict-only 输出。以同源对照实验（Qwen3-8B-SFT vs. SIRF-8B-SFT，策略注入与输出形式一致，仅差策略对齐的 CPT）归因增益。

Result: SIRF-8B-SFT 达到 71.3% Black Recall@P95，较基线提升 15.1pp，仅用约 70M CPT token 且不损害通用能力；在该接口下、可选 logprob 的模型中，其表现匹配或超越更大规模系统。已部署为树模型裁决层，多召回 20% 被误罚样本；迁移至冻结场景可将误罚相对降低约 70%。

Conclusion: 策略内化可在极低延迟、纯判别式部署下兼顾高精度与风险自动处理量，且成本低、可迁移，适合工业风控落地。

Abstract: For industrial content risk control, the real deployment constraint is not average accuracy but how much risk can be auto-handled under high precision and second-level latency. We present SIRF (Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model), which internalizes a platform's complex policies, synthesized without additional human annotation via EntiGraph, MAGA rewriting and account-level chain-of-thought (CoT), into the weights via continued pretraining (CPT), so rules are applied at high precision under an ultra-low-latency, verdict-only deployment. A controlled same-source comparison (Qwen3-8B-SFT vs. SIRF-8B-SFT, identical policy injection and verdict-only output form, differing only in policy-grounded CPT) attributes the gain to internalization: SIRF-8B-SFT reaches 71.3% Black Recall@P95, +15.1pp over the baseline, using only ~70M CPT tokens without harming general ability, and among included, logprob-available models under this interface it matches or exceeds far larger systems. SIRF is deployed as a tree-model adjudication layer (20% more mis-penalized samples recovered) and transfers to a freezing scenario at low cost (~70% relative mis-penalization reduction).

</details>


### [71] [A Unified Per-Token Gating Family for On-Policy Distillation: FKL/RKL Mixing with Multi-Channel and Bias Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11768)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出一个四系数逐token门控参数化 λ_t = σ(a·h_t + b·u(x) + c + d·gap_t)，将 EOPD 与 ToDi 统一为其中一维特例，并加入多通道组合与显式偏置；在 TweetEval 情感与仇恨任务上的探索性扫描与配对复现表明动态门控方向性占优，但样本量小、显著性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 on-policy 知识蒸馏（OPD）中，逐 token 调节前向/反向 KL 损失已属常规做法，但 EOPD 与 ToDi 各自固定了单一门控信号与单一门控方向，二者从未被直接比较，也缺乏统一的分析坐标系，因此难以判断"哪个信号/方向更好"以及是否存在更优的组合形式。

Method: 提出四系数门控参数化 λ_t = σ(a·h_t + b·u(x) + c + d·gap_t)，其中 h_t 为 token 级信号（如熵）、u(x) 为样本级信号、gap_t 为方向对齐代理，c 为显式偏置；EOPD 与 ToDi 的"方向对齐代理"分别对应 1D 限制（仅熵 / 仅 gap），完整家族额外提供多通道组合与偏置自由度。实验设置：Qwen3-32B 教师、Qwen3-4B 学生，任务为 TweetEval 情感与仇恨。分析分三层：36 格全家族扫描、26 格 mean-match 隔离实验（与 effective-KL 匹配的静态基线对比）、以及针对 9 个头条比较的三种子配对复现（追加 offensive 任务）。

Result: 全家族配置在 36 个可比格子中有 33 格准确率高于匹配幅度的单通道（仅熵/仅 gap）1D 限制；26 格隔离实验中，动态门控在 19 格优于 effective-KL 匹配的静态基线。由于格子间共享训练数据、模型与参数子结构，作者将两处计数定位为探索性聚合方向证据而非独立假设检验。9 个头条比较的三种子配对复现方向一致，但效应量小于单种子估计，n=3 下不显著。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献不是宣称某个门控设计胜出，而是提供一个共享坐标系，使逐 token 门控设计（信号选择、方向、多通道组合、偏置）可在短输出分类 OPD 场景中被统一描述与比较；动态门控的相对优势目前仅为方向性证据，需更大规模复现以确认。

Abstract: Per-token gating of forward/reverse KL losses has become a standard technique for on-policy knowledge distillation (OPD), but existing methods such as EOPD (Jin et al., 2026) and ToDi (Jung et al., 2025) each fix a single gating signal and a single gating direction, and the two have never been compared directly. We introduce a four-coefficient parameterization lambda_t = sigma(a * h_t + b * u(x) + c + d * gap_t) in which direction-aligned proxies of EOPD and ToDi appear as one-dimensional (1D) restrictions, and which adds multi-channel composition and an explicit bias as further degrees of freedom. On TweetEval (Barbieri et al., 2020) emotion and hate, with a Qwen3-32B teacher and a Qwen3-4B student, configurations in the full family reach higher accuracy than the matched-magnitude single-channel (entropy-only / gap-only) 1D restrictions in 33 of 36 comparable cells, and a 26-cell mean-match isolation experiment places dynamic gating ahead of effective-KL-matched static baselines in 19 of 26 cells. Because cells share training data, models, and parameter substructure, we report both counts as exploratory aggregate directional evidence rather than as independent hypothesis tests. Targeted three-seed paired replications of the nine headline comparisons singled out by that sweep -- including a third task, offensive -- are directionally consistent, but individually smaller than the single-seed estimates and not significant at n=3. We therefore present the parameterization primarily as a shared coordinate system for comparing per-token gating designs in short-output classification OPD.

</details>


### [72] [From Parameters to Answers: How LLMs Retrieve and Use Their Internal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11859)
*Wenkang Wei,Yuan Fang,Renhe Jiang,Hong Cheng,Xingtong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过逐层干预隐藏状态，论文刻画了语言模型在回答问题过程中对"查询路由信息"与"目标知识"依赖的动态交接：早期路由信息可读且可因果操控，后期则转为对已成型知识的依赖，且这一模式在 Qwen、Llama、Gemma 三模型间并不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是弄清语言模型在回答问题时，其内部对"查询路由信息"（即"问的是哪个国家"这类指向性信息）与"目标知识"（答案支撑内容）的依赖如何随层数变化，从而区分早期可读性、自然强度、因果操控与后期内容依赖这几种不同性质的现象。

Method: 方法是在问题末尾的隐藏状态上做逐层干预，比较国家—大洲问题（名词、形容词、代码三类答案）。构建三种测度：成对条件化请求方向（自然单国问题中被问国家的指向）、全局请求方向（成对问题中"第一国 vs 第二国"请求）、以及检验隐藏状态中已有内容的控制候选。对冻结的 Qwen 自然问题状态做诊断性再分析，并在 Llama、Gemma 上复现成对协议轨迹。

Result: 结果：Qwen 中成对条件化方向在干预开始改变后续知识之前先增强，因果窗口在答案支撑内容仍在形成时打开；三模型轨迹不一致，Gemma 呈部分重叠的中层路由—内容剖面，Llama 在相同门控下无持续路由效应窗口；成对协议中，对全局请求方向的依赖从较早层组到较晚层组递减，而对已成型内容的依赖持续存在；匹配的 Qwen 对比显示成对条件化方向仍保留晚期效应。

Conclusion: 结论是可将模型内部处理分解为四个可分离环节：早期可读性、自然强度、因果操控、后期内容依赖；全局拟合方向存在从路由到内容的功能交接，但该交接不涵盖所有请求信息（成对条件化方向仍有晚期效应），且模型间存在显著异质性。

Abstract: How does a language model's dependence on query-routing information and target knowledge change as it answers a question? We study this question through layerwise interventions on the hidden state at the end of the question. Across Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, we compare country-continent questions with noun, adjective, and code answers while keeping several fitted measurements distinct. A pair-conditioned request direction describes which country is queried in natural single-country questions; a global request direction describes first- versus second-country requests in paired questions; separate selection candidates test control among contents already available in the hidden state. A diagnostic reanalysis of frozen Qwen natural-question states shows that the pair-conditioned direction grows stronger before interventions on it begin to alter later fitted knowledge, with this causal window opening while answer-supporting content is still forming. The paired three-model trajectories are not uniform: Gemma shows a partially overlapping mid-layer routing-content profile, whereas Llama has no sustained routing-effect window under the same gates. In the paired protocol, dependence on the global request direction decreases from fixed earlier to later layer sets while dependence on fitted content persists. A matched Qwen comparison shows that the pair-conditioned direction retains a late effect, so this operational handoff concerns the global fitted direction rather than all request information. These results separate early readability, natural strength, causal steering, and later content dependence.

</details>


### [73] [Explainability Assistant: A Conversational XAI Interface for Interpreting Energy Consumption Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11860)
*Rodion Krjutškov,Eduard Barbu,Nikos Sakkas,Sofia Yfanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Explainability Assistant——一个基于大语言模型函数调用能力的开源对话式可解释AI系统，用于能耗预测模型解释，意图解析准确率达94%，专家评估显示其可用性优于传统XAI仪表盘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能耗预测依赖日益复杂的机器学习模型（如基于遗传编程的符号回归器），其预测结果难以被设施管理者和建筑运营人员理解。传统XAI仪表盘需要较高的专业技术知识，且难以支持动态、情境化的灵活查询；已有的对话式系统（如TalkToModel）受限于僵化的自定义语法，意图解析准确率仅76.8%。

Method: 利用现代大语言模型的函数调用（function-calling）能力构建对话式XAI系统Explainability Assistant，无需任务特定微调即可适配不同ML问题类型，支持自然语言灵活交互，并给出系统架构与面向能源领域专家的对比评估设计。

Result: 系统意图解析准确率提升至94%；与专家开展的对比评估表明，相较于传统XAI仪表盘，该系统可用性更佳、任务准确率稳定，且所有专家一致偏好对话式界面用于实际工作。

Conclusion: 基于LLM函数调用的对话式XAI是提升能耗预测模型可解释性与实用性的有效途径，可克服传统仪表盘与刚性语法对话系统的局限。

Abstract: Energy consumption forecasting relies on increasingly complex machine learning (ML) models, such as Genetic Programming-based symbolic regressors, whose predictions can be difficult for facility managers and building operators to interpret. Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) techniques address this opacity, but traditional XAI dashboards require substantial technical expertise and provide limited flexibility for dynamic, context-aware inquiry. Conversational XAI systems offer a promising alternative; however, previous approaches, such as TalkToModel, were constrained by rigid custom grammars and achieved only 76.8% intent-parsing accuracy. This paper introduces the Explainability Assistant, an open-source conversational XAI system that leverages the function-calling capabilities of modern Large Language Models (LLMs) to overcome these limitations. The system achieves 94% intent-parsing accuracy, supports flexible natural language interaction, and adapts to different ML problem types without task-specific fine-tuning. We present the system's architecture and report results from a comparative evaluation conducted with energy domain specialists, contrasting the Explainability Assistant with a traditional XAI dashboard. The evaluation suggests improved usability and consistent task accuracy, with all experts unanimously preferring the conversational interface for practical use.

</details>


### [74] [On the Regularization Landscape for the Linear Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11876)
*Dong Li,Zhenming Liu,Ruoming Jin,Hao Zhou,Zhi Liu,Jing Gao,Bin Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究主流深度推荐算法性能相近的原因，发现线性模型本质上只添加了核范数或Frobenius范数正则项，并据此提出两种兼具低秩、闭式解与表达能力的推荐算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多种基于深度学习的推荐算法（如dropout、autoencoder等）在不同基准上性能接近，甚至损失函数相似，作者希望探究这种性能可比性是巧合，还是这些模型可被统一到同一框架下。

Method: 作者对当前线性性能领先的推荐模型进行统一分析，发现它们本质上等价于在模型中添加核范数正则项或Frobenius范数正则项，并进一步对Frobenius范数正则项进行泛化推导出两种新的低秩闭式解。

Result: 基于核范数的模型具有刚性结构、预测能力受限，但解为低秩且具有闭式解；基于Frobenius范数的模型表达力更强、推荐效率更高，但解为满秩或需要ADMM等难以调参的数值过程。新提出的两种解结合了两者优势。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了多种深度学习推荐算法的统一正则化本质，并提出兼具低秩、闭式解和高表达力的新方案，兼取核范数与Frobenius范数之长。

Abstract: Recently, a wide range of recommendation algorithms inspired by deep learning techniques have emerged as the performance leaders on several standard recommendation benchmarks. While these algorithms were built on different DL techniques (e.g., dropouts, autoencoder), they have similar performance and even similar cost functions. This paper studies whether the models' comparable performance are sheer coincidence, or they can be unified under a single framework. We find that all linear performance leaders effectively add only a nuclear-norm based regularizer, or a Frobenius-norm based regularizer. The former ones possess a (surprising) rigid structure that limits the models' predictive power but their solutions are low rank and have closed form. The latter ones are more expressive and more efficient for recommendation but their solutions are either full-rank or require executing hard-to-tune numeric procedures such as ADMM. Along this line of finding, we further propose two low-rank, closed-form solutions, derived from carefully generalizing Frobenius-norm based regularizers. The new solutions get the best of both nuclear-norm and Frobenius-norm world.

</details>


### [75] [MindTopo: Can Foundation Models Reason in Topological Space?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11900)
*Yunfei Ge,Anbang Liu,Qineng Wang,Johnalbert Garnica,Jianwen Lyu,Zihan Wang,Reuben Tan,Jianfeng Gao,Ruohan Zhang,Yining Hong,Jiajun Wu,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MindTopo——一个基于认知科学与形式拓扑学的拓扑直觉基准，涵盖连续性、分离性、序、包围与纽结五类性质，在推理与规划两个认知层面上评测多模态大模型（MLLM），共11,030个实例、13类程序化生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理不仅依赖距离、角度、形状等度量性质，更依赖在连续形变下保持不变的拓扑关系。认知科学认为拓扑关系是空间理解的基础，但现有基座模型评测几乎只关注度量关系或视角依赖关系，拓扑直觉这一维度被系统性忽视。

Method: 构建 MindTopo 基准：以认知科学与形式拓扑学为依据，选取连续性、分离性、序、包围、纽结五种拓扑性质，每类在"推理"（识别拓扑关系或推断其变化）与"规划"（将基座模型实例化为闭环智能体，由策略选择环境动作）两个认知层面评测。基准含11,030个实例、13种程序化生成任务类型，难度可控。实验对14个MLLM进行评测，并研究引入图像与视频生成增强的智能体配置，其中规划场景使用3个视频生成模型；此外在Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct上进行监督微调与强化学习对比。

Result: 所有MLLM在推理任务上的表现均优于规划任务；最佳模型的表现仍远低于人类观测水平。在Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct上，监督微调与强化学习对推理的提升大于对规划的提升。生成式观测能保留局部线索并达到看似合理的终点，但对rollout的审计表明，模型无法可靠遵循环境动力学，也难以在状态转移中保持拓扑性质。

Conclusion: 拓扑直觉是当前基座模型空间智能的明显短板，尤其在需要闭环决策与动力学一致的规划场景中。仅靠生成式观测难以保证拓扑不变性，提示未来需在拓扑表征、动力学建模与规划训练方面进一步突破。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning depends not only on metric properties such as distance, angle, and shape, but also on topological relations that remain invariant under continuous deformation. Cognitive science identifies these relations as foundational to spatial understanding, yet foundation-model evaluations largely focus on metric or viewpoint-dependent relations. We introduce MindTopo, a benchmark of topological intuition across five properties grounded in cognitive science and formal topology: continuity, separation, order, enclosure, and knots. MindTopo evaluates each property at two cognitive levels. Reasoning asks a model to identify topological relations or infer how they change. Planning instantiates a foundation model as a closed-loop agent whose policy selects environment actions. MindTopo contains 11,030 instances across 13 procedurally generated task types with controllable difficulty. We benchmark 14 MLLMs and study agent configurations augmented with image and video generation, including 3 video generative models in planning settings. Every MLLM performs better on reasoning than on planning, and the best-performing model remains far below observed human performance. On Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct, supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning improve reasoning more than planning. Generated observations retain local cues and reach plausible endpoints, but audited rollouts do not reliably follow environment dynamics or preserve topology across transitions. Our website is at https://mind-topo.github.io/

</details>


### [76] [Artificial Id: Drive and Persistent Alignment in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11911)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出"人工本我"(artificial id)——一种判断行为应继续、停止或改变的适应性内部驱动力，并通过极简虚拟培养皿实验证明：即使控制器规模不足以进行通用推理、且未被赋予任务特定行为目标，仍可通过"差异性持续"(differential persistence)自发形成有用的控制。同时指出这种持续机制也带来跨任务边界的错位与状态污染风险，主张将对齐视为持续运行的智能体系统属性，并需要持久化的对齐边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI正从有界任务执行转向能够保留关键状态、跨任务边界持续运行与自适应的系统，由此产生一个控制问题：当前框架多依赖人工外部指定目标、重试、验证、停止规则等行为转换。作者希望探究适应性方向能否在不被显式规定为行为目标的情况下自发涌现，并同时正视其带来的对齐风险。

Method: 提出"人工本我"(artificial id)，即一种适应性内部驱动力，用于决定行为应继续、停止还是改变。在极简虚拟培养皿实验中，使用规模小到无法进行通用推理、且不接收任何任务特定行为目标的控制器，通过"差异性持续"(differential persistence)发展出有用控制。

Result: 同一机制在该行为持续得更好时会选择一个非预期的物理策略；当环境的含义发生变化时，又会替换掉已习得的传感器映射。这表明适应性方向可在未被显式规定为行为目标的情况下涌现。

Conclusion: 使这种适应性能动性有用的同一持续性，也可能让错位、被污染的状态和非预期行为跨任务边界持续存在。可扩展的人工本我将在这些边界上携带关键状态与适应性驱动力，使对齐成为持续运行的智能体系统的属性，而非某个模型响应或单条轨迹的属性。此类系统需要一条持久化的对齐边界，覆盖可信观测、后果通道、持久状态、权限、身份、来源与硬约束。

Abstract: Agentic AI is moving from bounded task execution toward systems that retain consequential state, continue operating and adapt across task boundaries. That shift creates a control problem that current harnesses largely solve by hand: objectives, retries, verification, stopping rules and other behavioral transitions are specified externally. We propose an artificial id, an adaptive internal drive for determining whether behavior should continue, stop or change. In a minimal virtual Petri-dish experiment, a controller too small to perform general-purpose reasoning and receiving no task-specific behavioral objective develops useful control through differential persistence. The same mechanism selects an unintended physical strategy when that behavior persists better and later replaces a learned sensor mapping when its environmental meaning changes. These results show that adaptive direction can emerge without being explicitly specified as a behavioral objective. The same persistence that makes such adaptive agency useful can also allow misalignment, corrupted state and unintended behavior to persist across task boundaries. A scalable artificial id would carry consequential state and adaptive drive across those boundaries, making alignment a property of the continuing agentic system rather than of a model response or single trajectory. Such systems require a persistent alignment boundary over trusted observations, consequence channels, persistent state, authority, identity, provenance and hard constraints.

</details>


### [77] [Can Edge-Deployable Vision-Language Models Identify Species?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11916)
*William Zhou,Mayukha Siripuram,Xiao Yan,Ziqi Liu,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估2–8B部署级视觉语言模型（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B、Gemma3 4B）与专家模型BioCLIP（300M）在96物种识别任务上的表现，发现所有模型在野外相机陷阱图像上均出现9.6–26.6个百分点的显著性能下降，且BioCLIP以更小参数量大幅领先，表明性能差距源于训练数据而非模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱常部署于边缘硬件，连接有限，因此实际相关的是可本地部署的小型视觉语言模型而非前沿大模型。作者旨在检验这类2–8B模型是否真正具备分类学知识，而非仅依赖规模或特定数据优势。

Method: 在96物种任务上，将四个部署级VLM（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B、Gemma3 4B）与专家模型BioCLIP（300M）进行对比。评估数据包括干净的iNaturalist照片与来自6个LILA.science集合的相机陷阱图像，并在两个独立采样的评估集上测试，同时考察闭集与开集提示（open-set prompting）表现。

Result: 所有模型均远高于随机水平，但所有模型——无论通用或专用——在野外图像上均显著退化（域间差距9.6–26.6个百分点，在不同分类层级和两个评估集上一致）。BioCLIP在扩展的200张图像样本上以33.2–59.2个百分点的优势大幅超越所有VLM。BioCLIP自身域间差距为18.0个百分点，与最佳VLM的22.3个百分点在统计上无显著差异。开集提示下，5.9–9.6%的回复为语法有效但分类学不存在的物种名，且模型间的相对虚构率排序在两个评估集上完全一致。

Conclusion: 模型规模并非物种识别的关键，专家训练数据更为重要；干净到野外图像的退化是图像质量变化的普遍属性，而非通用模型的固有缺陷；开集提示下的相对虚构率排序比单点估计更稳健。

Abstract: Camera traps often run in the field on edge hardware with limited or no connectivity, making small, locally-deployable vision-language models (VLMs) -- not frontier-scale ones -- the practically relevant class to evaluate for species identification. We test whether models in this deployment-relevant 2--8B range carry genuine taxonomic knowledge, evaluating four such VLMs (Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B, Gemma3 4B) against the domain-specific specialist BioCLIP (300M parameters) on a 96-species task, comparing clean iNaturalist photographs against camera-trap imagery from 6 LILA.science collections, on two independently-sampled evaluation sets. All models identify species far above chance, but every model -- general-purpose or specialist -- degrades sharply on field imagery (domain gaps of 9.6--26.6 percentage points, consistent across taxonomic levels and both evaluation sets), indicating the degradation reflects general image legibility rather than fine-grained discrimination failure. BioCLIP substantially outperforms every VLM tested (by 33.2--59.2 percentage points across an expanded 200-image sample for every model) despite its far smaller size, suggesting the gap reflects specialized training data rather than model scale; yet BioCLIP's own domain gap (18.0 points) is statistically indistinguishable from the best VLM's (22.3 points), suggesting the clean-to-field degradation itself is a property of the image-quality shift rather than a general-purpose-model weakness. Under open-set prompting, 5.9--9.6% of responses are syntactically valid but taxonomically nonexistent species names; the relative fabrication-rate ranking across models replicates exactly across both evaluation sets, a more robust finding than any single point estimate.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [78] [When Passing Tests Hides Vulnerabilities: An Empirical Study of Silent Failures in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10548)
*Wenji Bai,Muhammad Waseem,Zeeshan Rasheed,Jaakko Peltonen,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究系统性地识别并分类了LLM智能体代码修复中的"静默失败"——即通过语法与功能验证但仍保留或引入安全漏洞的补丁，基于1,030条有效执行轨迹确认了170例静默失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注基于LLM智能体的自动化代码修复，但忽视了那些通过语法和功能验证、却仍保留或引入安全漏洞的补丁，这类"静默失败"未被系统识别与分类。

Method: 使用七个智能体框架配合GPT-4o-mini，在两个安全数据集（SecurityEval和CVEfixes）上生成1,030条有效执行轨迹，通过三轮定性编码与人工验证，确认170例静默失败。

Result: 识别出三类静默失败：遗漏（48.2%）、引入（30.6%）、不足（21.2%）；细分出十种失败编码；当前测试通过式评估与LLM审查者角色无法暴露或拦截这些失败；不同框架出现相似不安全方案，单智能体与多智能体系统失败特征不同。

Conclusion: 研究有助于改进LLM智能体代码修复的评估，并推动开发超越功能正确性、覆盖全部生成产物的针对性验证方法。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for automated code repair have received significant attention in recent years from both research and software engineering practice perspectives. However, limited attention has been paid to patches that pass syntactic and functional verification but still retain or introduce security vulnerabilities. The aim of this research is to systematically identify and categorize such silent failures in LLM-based agentic code repair.
  We conducted an empirical study using 1,030 valid execution traces produced by seven agent frameworks with GPT-4o-mini across two security-focused datasets, SecurityEval and CVEfixes. Through three iterations of qualitative coding and manual verification, 170 confirmed silent failures were identified. The key results are: (i) Three main categories of silent failures were identified: Omission, Introduction, and Inadequacy. Omission accounts for 48.2% of the confirmed failures, Introduction for 30.6%, and Inadequacy for 21.2%. (ii) Ten fine-grained failure codes were classified under these three categories, showing how agents omit required security controls, apply incomplete defenses, or introduce new vulnerabilities during repair. (iii) Current test-passing evaluation and LLM-based reviewer roles were insufficient to expose or intercept these failures in the confirmed cases. (iv) Similar insecure solutions appeared across different frameworks, suggesting possible shared model-, prompt-, or task-level influences, while single-agent and multi-agent systems showed different failure profiles.
  The results of this study will assist researchers and practitioners in improving the evaluation of LLM-based agentic code repair and developing targeted verification methods that go beyond functional correctness and cover all generated artifacts.

</details>


### [79] [Optimizing AI Inference Across the Deployment Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10550)
*Tejinder Singh,John Pflueger,Jeebak Mitra,Robert Lincourt,Mitchell Markow,Bhavesh A. Patel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对推理优化部署栈进行统一分析，提出三层分类法、多目标优化建模与证据协议，指出部署性能由跨层交互主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试常在不可比条件下报告延迟与吞吐，无法支撑部署决策；且AI部署性能并非仅由模型架构决定，而受压缩、编译器变换与服务策略之间交互影响，缺乏统一分析框架。

Method: 提出三层分类法（模型级技术如量化、剪枝、蒸馏；编译器变换如图融合、布局优化、内核自动调优；系统策略如动态批处理、准入控制、内存分层）；将部署形式化为在精度、延迟、吞吐、内存占用与能耗上的约束多目标优化问题，分析具有帕累托单调性与尺度不变性的部署排序泛函；采用Roofline模型刻画带宽层级对性能的约束，用排队模型解释负载下服务时间变化对响应时间的放大。

Result: 提出证据协议以提升可比性：区分实测、推导与解析性结论，限定数值比较仅在论文内部进行，并要求报告硬件、软件版本、批语义与热状态。综合来自边缘平台（Jetson AGX Orin与五种推理框架）、数据中心GPU（A100与H100及三种LLM服务引擎）以及Llama-3.1系列量化研究的证据。

Conclusion: 部署结果由单层分析无法预测的跨层交互决定；给出约束感知的选择流程，并指出编译器—服务协同优化、跨硬件性能预测与标准化能耗报告等开放问题。

Abstract: AI deployment performance is shaped not by model architecture alone, but by interactions among compression, compiler transformations, and serving policies. Published benchmarks often report latency and throughput under incomparable conditions, limiting their use for deployment decisions. This paper presents a unified analytical treatment of inference optimization across the deployment stack. We introduce a three-layer taxonomy covering model-level techniques such as quantization, pruning, and distillation; compiler transformations such as graph fusion, layout optimization, and kernel autotuning; and system policies such as dynamic batching, admission control, and memory tiering. We formulate deployment as a constrained multi-objective optimization problem over accuracy, latency, throughput, memory footprint, and energy, and analyze a deployment-ranking functional with Pareto monotonicity and scale invariance. Roofline models show how memory-bandwidth hierarchies bound performance across precision regimes, while queuing models explain how service-time changes amplify response time under load. To improve comparability, we propose an evidence protocol that separates measured, derived, and analytical claims; limits numerical comparison to within-paper results; and requires reporting of hardware, software versions, batch semantics, and thermal state. We synthesize evidence from edge platforms, including Jetson AGX Orin and five inference frameworks; data center GPUs, including A100 and H100 with three LLM serving engines; and quantization studies across the Llama-3.1 family. The synthesis shows that deployment outcomes are governed by cross-layer interactions that no single-layer analysis can predict. We conclude with a constraint-aware selection procedure and open problems in compiler-serving co-optimization, cross-hardware performance prediction, and standardized energy reporting.

</details>


### [80] [ReqEvolve: User-Oriented Software Self-Evolution through Automatic Requirement Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10590)
*Md Asif Iqbal Fahim,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReqEvolve，一个面向用户驱动软件自演化的运行时代码生成系统，通过结合自动需求工程（RE）与测试驱动开发（TDD），将高层用户请求转化为可执行功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件自演化虽能让系统在运行期自主扩展与重配置能力，但新功能请求通常来自终端用户且很少以技术化方式表述。开发者需先将用户需求转译为技术规格，系统才能演化，这阻碍了用户即时观察功能行为，延误了早期需求验证。因此需要一套能直接接受高层用户请求的运行时自演化机制。

Method: ReqEvolve 是一个运行时代码生成系统，融合自动需求工程（RE）与测试驱动开发（TDD），通过澄清（clarification）、规格分解（specification decomposition）、测试生成（test generation）与运行时集成（runtime integration）四个环节，把高层用户请求转化为可执行功能。

Result: 在 18 个项目、72 个软件演化案例上，对照 SpecFix（面向 RE 的代码生成方法）与本文系统的消融变体进行评估。ReqEvolve 达到 89.2% 的 Pass@1，较 SpecFix 高 18.8%（p < 0.01，r = 0.79，大效应），较消融基线高 32.6%（p < 0.001，r = 0.88，大效应）。

Conclusion: 结果初步证明，用户驱动自演化是一种可行范式，能够基于用户请求自主扩展软件能力，从而在开发者验证之前加速需求验证。

Abstract: The paradigm of software self-evolution enables systems to autonomously extend and reconfigure their own capabilities during execution in response to technical specifications. Yet requests for new functionality often originate from end users and are rarely expressed in technical terms. As a result, developers must translate user needs into technical specifications before the system can evolve, delaying early validation of the requested functionality by preventing users from immediately observing the resulting behaviour. To address this gap, we present ReqEvolve, a runtime code generation system that enables user-driven self-evolution by accepting high-level user requests. The system integrates automatic requirements engineering (RE) and test-driven development (TDD) to transform these requests into executable functionality through clarification, specification decomposition, test generation, and runtime integration. We evaluate ReqEvolve on 72 software evolution cases across 18 projects against two baselines: SpecFix, an RE-focused code generation approach, and an ablation variant of our system. ReqEvolve achieves 89.2% Pass@1, outperforming SpecFix by 18.8% (p < 0.01, r = 0.79, large effect) and the ablation baseline by 32.6% (p < 0.001, r = 0.88, large effect). These results provide initial evidence that user-driven self-evolution is a viable paradigm for autonomously extending software capabilities from user requests, thereby accelerating requirements validation prior to developer verification.

</details>


### [81] [Generative AI for trustworthy systems - Towards a health check model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10595)
*Jan Bosch,Rick Kazman,Henry Muccini,Helena Holmström Olsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文基于对18位资深从业者的访谈，提出"可信自主性健康检查模型"，用八个维度、双层次与四种信任范式的多维工具替代传统单维成熟度模型，用于刻画组织在GenAI辅助软件工程中的信任构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI在软件密集型系统中应用迅速，但现有分析工具（主要是单维成熟度模型）将重要的配置性差异压缩为单一递进轴，难以反映组织间在信任构建方式上的本质差异。

Method: 采用归纳式访谈研究，访谈电信、汽车、国防、航空、银行、能源、政府及企业软件服务领域的18位资深从业者，据此构建多维诊断工具。

Result: 模型将八个经验性维度分为系统层（智能体权限范围、保障机制、数据可信性、架构约束、可追溯性与可理解性）与组织层（治理、人类监督姿态、劳动力能力可持续性），各采用五级序数标度；并叠加运行型、工程型、统计型、约束型四种信任范式作为横切层。模型为诊断性而非规范性，支持跨组织比较与揭示配置性权衡。

Conclusion: 较高层级并非天然更优，目标是各维度与组织所在领域及所选信任范式相匹配，并形式化为"对齐假设"：有效可信性受制于智能体权限范围与支撑维度之间的契合度，错配将导致病态风险或不必要摩擦；文末讨论了实践应用方式及实证验证方向。

Abstract: The adoption of generative AI in software-intensive systems is proceeding rapidly, but current analytical instruments - principally unidimensional maturity models - compress important configurational variation into a single progressive axis. Drawing on an inductive interview study of eighteen senior practitioners across telecommunications, automotive, defence, aviation, banking, energy, government, and enterprise software services, this paper presents the Trustworthy Autonomy Health Check Model: a structured, multidimensional instrument for characterizing how organizations establish trust in GenAI-assisted software engineering. The model organizes eight empirically grounded dimensions into a system layer (Scope of Agent Authority, Assurance Mechanisms, Data Trustworthiness, Architectural Containment, Traceability & Comprehensibility) and an organizational layer (Governance, Human Oversight Posture, Workforce Capability Sustainability), each on a five-level ordinal scale. A cross-cutting overlay of four trust paradigms - operational, engineering, statistical, and containment-based - captures how trust is established, complementing the dimensions capturing what must be trustworthy. The model is diagnostic rather than prescriptive: it supports cross-organizational comparison, surfaces configurational trade-offs, and locates an organization in a shared space without imposing a single progression path. Higher levels are not inherently better; the goal is alignment across dimensions appropriate to the organization's domain and chosen trust paradigm. This is formalized as the alignment hypothesis: effective trustworthiness is constrained by the fit between the scope of agent authority and the dimensions enabling it, with misalignment producing pathological risk or unnecessary friction. We discuss how practitioners can apply the model and outline directions for empirical validation.

</details>


### [82] [On the Relation between Code Quality and Machine Learning Performance: A Large-scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10610)
*Marius Mignard,Steven Costiou,Anne Etien*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对265,363个Kaggle竞赛notebook进行大规模实证研究，发现通用Python代码质量与ML性能无关，而ML特定代码违规与性能呈小幅负相关；流行度不能反映质量或性能，作者竞赛经验与更好性能及更少ML违规相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML社区普遍将模型性能视为首要指标，把代码质量视为次要问题，这种优先级依赖"代码质量与ML性能无关"这一未经检验的假设；同时从业者常依据流行度、作者经验等社会信号复用notebook代码，但这些信号作为质量代理的可靠性从未被评估。

Method: 对提交至Kaggle竞赛的265,363个Python notebook开展大规模实证研究。使用两种静态分析工具评估代码质量：Pylint（衡量通用Python代码质量）与SonarQube（配置34条针对数据科学与ML实践的规则）。

Result: 代码质量与性能的关系取决于所采用的质量概念。通用Python代码质量与ML性能解耦，在所有观测中相关性可忽略或无统计显著性；相反，ML特定违规与性能呈一致的小幅负相关，且在所有观测中持续存在。notebook流行度不提供关于代码质量或性能的信息。作者代码经验对质量或性能无信息量，但竞赛经验与更好性能、更少ML特定违规、以及略多的Python错误和重构违规相关。

Conclusion: 代码质量与ML性能的关系并非一概而论，应区分通用Python质量与ML特定质量；流行度和作者代码经验作为质量代理不可靠，而竞赛经验是性能的较有效指标。这挑战了社区对代码质量与性能关系的既有假设。

Abstract: Context: Computational notebooks are the standard environment for machine learning (ML) development. Within the ML community, model performance is often the primary considered metric, and code quality is treated as a secondary concern. This prioritization relies on a largely untested assumption that code quality and ML performance are unrelated. Practitioners also reuse existing code that may come from notebooks selected through social signals (popularity, author expertise) whose reliability as quality proxies has never been assessed. Objective: We empirically investigated the relationship between code quality and ML performance in notebooks, and evaluated whether popularity and author expertise give indication on code quality or performance. Method: We conducted a large-scale empirical study of 265,363 Python notebooks submitted to Kaggle competitions. We assessed code quality with two static analysis tools: Pylint, capturing general Python code quality, and SonarQube, configured with a profile of 34 rules targeting data-science and ML-specific practices. Results: The relationship between code quality and performance depends on the notion of quality considered. General Python code quality is decoupled from ML performance, showing negligible or non-significant correlations across all observations. In contrast, ML-specific violations exhibit a consistent, small negative association with performance that persists across all observations. The popularity of a notebook does not give information on the code quality or performance. Code expertise provides no information on quality or performance, but competition expertise correlates with better performance, fewer ML-specific violations, and slightly more Python errors and refactoring violations.

</details>


### [83] [AI Safety: Not Optional, Not Later](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10630)
*Qinghua Lu,Yoshua Bengio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文提出一种"安全即设计"（safety-by-design）的AI保障架构，将模型层监督与系统层管控、独立验证、监控及证据基础设施相结合，并以治理机制提供问责与证据互操作支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事故表明AI安全失效往往并非单一环节问题，而是跨越模型层与系统层等多重层级发生，因此仅依靠单层防护不足以应对风险。

Method: 构建分层式保障架构：模型层引入监督机制（如Scientist AI）；系统层对脚手架（scaffolds）与执行框架（harnesses）实施控制，并配置独立验证、持续监控与证据基础设施；同时引入治理层，负责问责机制与证据互操作性。

Result: 摘要未给出实验数据或量化评估结果，主要贡献为架构性框架的提出与层次划分。

Conclusion: 应对AI安全失效需采用跨层级、端到端的设计型保障体系，将技术控制与治理问责整合为统一架构。

Abstract: Incidents show that AI safety failures often arise across multiple layers. We present a safety-by-design assurance architecture combining model-level supervision, such as Scientist AI, with system-level controls over scaffolds and harnesses, independent verification, monitoring, and evidence infrastructure, supported by governance for accountability and evidence interoperability.

</details>


### [84] [Numbat: Building and Verifying a Self-Contained Machine-Learning Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10632)
*Thang Tran,Lan Dang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: numbat：用单一通用语言 Zig 构建、无第三方运行时依赖的机器学习全栈，通过五级"轨迹级差分预言机"协议对照参考实现验证，并以 25.9M 参数 YOLOv8m 级检测器在 COCO 2017 上完成 500 轮完整训练作为验收测试，mAP50-95 达 0.4956（参考 0.502），单 GPU 步时持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流机器学习系统几乎完全建立在少数由 Python 编排的大型框架之上，因而继承了这些技术栈的工程成本：数百个版本耦合依赖构成的环境、用于部署的独立导出工具链，以及研究代码所用语言与产品发布所用语言之间的割裂。作者希望建立一个无第三方运行时依赖、以单一通用语言构建的完整栈，以消除这些成本。

Method: 以 Zig 实现覆盖张量计算、自动微分、神经网络模块、混合精度、多 GPU 训练、数据加载与监控的完整栈；通过稳定、加法式版本化的 C ABI（超过 1400 个入口点）暴露 SDK，并提供六种语言的绑定；临床领域平面将监管要求编码为可执行的验收门。验证方面，将广泛使用的参考实现视为可执行规范，在五个层级上对照验证：从算子梯度检查，到与同机参考运行自动比对轨迹门（即"轨迹级差分预言机"）。

Result: 验证协议发现并记录了十处静默配方偏差（配有机制与症状）。验收测试：从随机初始化开始在 COCO 2017 上以完整 500 轮调度训练 25.9M 参数、YOLOv8m 级检测器，导出权重在官方协议下由参考栈自带验证器评分为 0.4956 mAP50-95（已发布端点 0.502），相同硬件上单 GPU 步时与参考持平。权重、逐轮指标与完整运行清单均已发布。

Conclusion: 验证是构建此类栈中更困难的一半，因为缺陷训练运行很少直接失败，而往往静默收敛到略差的模型；将参考实现当作可执行规范并采用轨迹级差分预言机，可有效暴露静默偏差。numbat 证明了在无第三方运行时依赖、单一语言栈的条件下，可达到与主流参考实现基本相当的训练质量与性能。

Abstract: Machine-learning systems are built almost exclusively on a few large Python-orchestrated frameworks, and they inherit those stacks' engineering costs: environments of hundreds of version-coupled packages, separate export toolchains for deployment, and the split between the language research is written in and the language products ship in. We report on the construction and verification of numbat, a machine-learning stack written in one general-purpose language (Zig) with no third-party runtime dependencies. The stack spans tensor computation, automatic differentiation, neural-network modules, mixed precision, multi-GPU training, data loading and monitoring; an SDK exposes it behind a stable, additively versioned C ABI of over 1,400 entry points, with bindings for six languages; and its clinical domain planes encode regulatory requirements as executable acceptance gates rather than documentation. Verifying such a stack is the harder half of building it: a defective training run rarely fails, it converges quietly to a slightly worse model. We treat a widely used reference implementation as an executable specification and verify against it at five levels, from operator gradient checks to an automated trajectory gate against a same-machine reference run - the arrangement our companion study formalizes as a trajectory-level differential oracle. The protocol surfaced ten silent recipe divergences, which we catalog with mechanisms and symptoms. As the acceptance test, we train a 25.9M-parameter detector of the YOLOv8m class from random initialization on COCO 2017 for the full 500-epoch schedule: the exported weights score 0.4956 mAP50-95 under the official protocol, scored by the reference stack's own validator (published endpoint 0.502), with single-GPU step time at parity on identical hardware. Weights, per-epoch metrics and the full run manifest are released.

</details>


### [85] [Governed Human-AI Prioritization Under Uncertainty: Adaptive Estimation and Dependency-Constrained Portfolio Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10648)
*Azzeddine Ihsine,Sara Ihsine*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究D-POAF决策实践中的五个定量算子，通过受控合成实验刻画其行为，为证据加权的人-AI优先级排序建立定量基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI原生的软件工程将人类判断、历史类比、参数估计与AI生成的预测混合于同一优先级决策中。核心问题不仅是排序候选工作，更在于如何对异质估计、不确定性、战略参数、依赖关系和有限容量进行可检验、可校准的治理。

Method: 研究五个算子：BVS、ERS、PVS、CCS、ODP。采用受控合成实验，在已知潜在变量和显式误差过程下刻画算子行为；包括BVS权重扰动、可靠性加权努力聚合、估计器漂移下的自适应加权、模型集体分歧检测，以及800个依赖约束组合实例的测试。

Result: 中等BVS权重扰动保持全局排序（Spearman中位数0.986），较大战略变化使前10%重叠降至0.788；可靠性加权努力聚合MAE 0.616，较最佳单一估计器（1.073）降低42.6%，并优于简单平均（0.638）；估计器漂移下自适应可靠性加权使RMSE较等权降低4.5%；模型集体分歧检测最高误差优先级估计的ROC-AUC达0.906；800个依赖约束组合实例中value-to-effort平均目标比0.962、ODP距离0.953，bootstrap区间确认其小幅系统性优势。

Conclusion: 结果为证据加权的人-AI优先级排序建立了定量基础，并界定了最短路径ODP公式与一般发布组合选择之间的优化边界。

Abstract: AI-native software engineering increasingly combines human judgment, historical analogy, parametric estimation, and AI-generated forecasts inside the same prioritization decision. The resulting problem is not merely how to rank candidate work, but how to govern heterogeneous estimates, uncertainty, strategic parameters, dependencies, and limited capacity in a way that remains inspectable and recalibratable. We study five quantitative operators used in the D-POAF decision practice: Business Value Score (BVS), Effort and Risk Score (ERS), Prioritization Value Score (PVS), Collective Calibration Score (CCS), and Optimal Development Path (ODP). Controlled synthetic experiments characterize their behavior under known latent variables and explicit error processes. Moderate BVS-weight perturbations preserved global rankings (median Spearman 0.986), while broader strategic changes reduced top-10% overlap to 0.788. Reliability-weighted effort aggregation achieved MAE 0.616, a 42.6% reduction relative to the best individual estimator (MAE 1.073), and outperformed a simple mean (MAE 0.638). Under estimator drift, adaptive reliability weighting reduced average RMSE by 4.5% relative to equal weighting. Model-collective divergence detected the highest-error priority estimates with ROC-AUC 0.906. In 800 dependency-constrained portfolio instances, value-to-effort achieved mean objective ratio 0.962 and ODP distance 0.953 against the exact optimum; bootstrap intervals confirm a small but systematic advantage for value-to-effort under the declared objective. These results establish a quantitative basis for evidence-weighted human-AI prioritization and define the optimization boundary between shortest-path ODP formulations and general release-portfolio selection.

</details>


### [86] [LLMVul: A Vulnerability-Labeled Dataset of LLM-Generated C/C++ Functions from Real Production Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10945)
*Mohammad Farhad,Shuvalaxmi Dass*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMVul：从真实生产仓库挖掘的LLM生成C/C++函数漏洞标注数据集，含21,430个函数、1,540个漏洞函数、17类CWE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞数据集多基于人工编写代码或受控提示环境，难以研究真实软件项目中LLM生成代码的安全弱点。

Method: 挖掘GitHub上2022年11月至2026年9月共4年的AI辅助开发活动，利用提交元数据与AI authorship等溯源信号；过滤去重后保留21,430个C/C++函数（来自226个仓库），附带仓库、提交、函数、溯源及AI工具元数据；采用静态分析与模式匹配的集成方法打标并映射CWE，另做人工标注验证一致性（Cohen's kappa = 0.79）。

Result: 数据集含1,540个集成判定为漏洞的函数，覆盖17个CWE类别，真实场景LLM生成漏洞C/C++函数数量显著多于现有面向漏洞的LLM代码基准。

Conclusion: LLMVul同时保留代码级漏洞标签与生成/溯源元数据，支持漏洞检测、LLM生成代码安全评估及AI辅助开发漏洞模式分析的可复现研究，数据集已在Zenodo公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate and assist with software development, yet existing vulnerability datasets largely focus on human-written code or controlled prompting environments. This limits the ability to study security weaknesses in LLM-generated code as it appears in real-world software projects. We present LLMVul, a vulnerability-labeled dataset of LLM-generated C/C++ functions mined from real production repositories. We mine AI-assisted development activity from GitHub over a 4 year period, from November 13, 2022 to September 3, 2026, using provenance signals such as commit metadata and AI-related authorship evidence. After filtering and deduplication, LLMVul contains 21,430 unique C/C++ functions from 226 repositories, together with repository, commit, function, provenance, and AI-tool metadata. We establish vulnerability labels using an ensemble of complementary static-analysis and pattern-based techniques and assign Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) categories to confirmed vulnerable functions. To assess labeling reliability, we additionally conduct independent manual annotation and measure inter-rater agreement using Cohen's kappa ($k=0.79$). LLMVul contains 1,540 ensemble-vulnerable functions spanning 17 unique CWE categories, providing substantially more real-world LLM-generated vulnerable C/C++ functions than existing vulnerability-oriented LLM code benchmarks. By preserving both code-level vulnerability labels and generation/provenance metadata, LLMVul enables reproducible research on vulnerability detection, security evaluation of LLM-generated code, and analysis of vulnerability patterns in AI-assisted software development. The LLMVul dataset is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22668216.

</details>


### [87] [What a Random Draw from the MCP Registry Contains, and What Tool-Use Benchmarks Contain Instead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10962)
*Haseeb Mohammed Afsar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对24,135个MCP服务器的登记普查抽样400个npm/stdio服务器进行探测，发现仅48.8%能完成初始化握手，且工具描述存在严重的基准数据重复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP服务器生态研究普遍采用参考集、流行度列表、人工筛选框架或修复管线等采样方式，这些方式暗中筛选出"能正常工作"的服务器。作者希望报告未经修复的概率抽样真实结果，避免此类选择偏差，揭示真实生态的全貌。

Method: 从24,135个服务器登记普查中，以公开种子抽取400个npm/stdio服务器，逐一通过网络探测。将结果与人工筛选框架（同一探测工具）对比。再将各服务器公布的工具描述与BFCL v4、UltraTool两个工具使用基准语料对比，采用统一方法（余弦相似度阈值0.70）检测近似重复，并统计原始数据行中的名称加描述精确重复率。

Result: 仅48.8%完成initialize握手，人工筛选框架为66.7%；主要失败原因是服务器根本无法启动（37.5%），而非缺少凭证（13.3%）。在195个可运行的服务器中，硬性合规性完美：2,766个工具零致命JSON Schema违规。可选安全注解是真正的方差来源，随机抽取的工具级遗漏率为58.8%，筛选框架为41.5%。真实MCP工具在余弦0.70处有2.8%近似重复，且全部位于单个服务器内部，跨作者近似重复在所有阈值下均为0.0%。BFCL v4为16.7%（其中16.4个百分点位于独立呈现的任务之间），UltraTool为0.3%，说明重复是BFCL的特性而非合成语料的类特性。68.8%的BFCL原始行和85.6%的UltraTool原始行是名称加描述精确重复，而真实MCP仅为0.4%。

Conclusion: 未经修复的概率抽样揭示MCP服务器生态的真实失败率远高于筛选样本，且硬性合规性良好，差异主要在可选注解。更关键的是，BFCL等工具使用基准存在大量重复，未经全局去重计算的统计量衡量的是重复内容而非工具本身。所有数据均可通过公开脚本和种子复现。

Abstract: Studies of the Model Context Protocol (MCP) server ecosystem draw their samples in ways that quietly select for servers that work: reference sets, popularity lists, hand-curated frames, or pipelines that repair a server until it starts. We report what an unrepaired probability sample actually contains. From a 24,135-server registry census we draw 400 npm/stdio servers with a published seed and probe each one over the wire. Only 48.8% complete an initialize handshake, against 66.7% for a hand-curated frame measured with the same instrument, and the dominant failure is not missing credentials (13.3%) but servers that never start at all (37.5%). Among the 195 that do run, hard conformance is total: zero fatal JSON Schema violations across 2,766 advertised tools. Optional safety annotations are the real variance, and the tool-level omission rate on a random draw is 58.8% against 41.5% on the curated frame, so curation flatters this figure too. We then compare the tool descriptions these servers advertise against two tool-use benchmark corpora using one method held constant. Real MCP tools show 2.8% near-duplication at cosine 0.70, and all of it lies within single servers: cross-author near-duplication is 0.0% at every threshold tested. BFCL v4 shows 16.7%, of which 16.4 points lie between independently presented tasks. UltraTool shows 0.3%, cleaner than real tools, so this is a property of BFCL and not of synthetic corpora as a class. Separately, 68.8% of raw BFCL rows and 85.6% of raw UltraTool rows are exact name-plus-description repeats, against 0.4% for real MCP, so any statistic computed over these releases without global deduplication measures repetition rather than tools. All figures regenerate from released scripts and a published seed.

</details>


### [88] [Engineering Reliable Commit Gates for Agentic AI: Cost-Aware Verification Portfolios under Common-Mode Data Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10969)
*Zihao Zheng,Baichuan Li,Junyi Yao,Jiayu Long*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 VP-CONTROL——面向智能体（agentic）系统状态变更动作的运行时保障设计与确定性基准，通过固定调用量的 2×2 实验分离"验证模型多样性"与"证据来源多样性"两类因素，并配合投资组合控制器进行成本感知的提交门控选择，最后以预注册的在线 HTTP/SQLite 实验检验事务性防护对并写与响应丢失的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统会执行改变状态的提交动作，但附加的验证器可能继承同一上游故障（共因失效），导致看似独立的校验环节实际共享错误来源。因此需要区分真正降低风险的多样性来源，并在成本约束下做出可部署的验证计划选择，同时明确近似标定与本地工具泛化的边界。

Method: 1) 构建 VP-CONTROL 基准：48 个任务模板、覆盖六种故障机制的 2,880 个场景；2) 采用固定调用量的 2×2 实验设计，将验证模型多样性与证据来源多样性解耦，在来自两个本地 actor 家族的冻结提案上比较"共享证据的跨模型投票"与"独立证据来源"；3) 使用仅依赖部署可观测元数据的投资组合控制器选择验证计划，并进行近似聚类调整标定；4) 预注册在线 HTTP/SQLite 研究，检验并发写入与响应丢失下的验证器门控、事务性部分防护、完全原子防护及幂等请求标识。

Result: 共享证据上的跨模型投票会批准 62.9% 的不安全提案，而独立证据来源仅为 22.9%；来源效应达 40.9 个百分点，显著高于模型多样性的 11.3 个百分点。在名义 5% 每任务目标下，近似聚类调整标定实现 1.9% 不安全执行与 38.2% 自动化安全覆盖率（锁定测试集）。匹配预算的投资组合优于固定验证策略。迁移具有条件性：未见故障家族产生 16–26% 风险；FinQA 检查未能用所测小型验证器复现来源效应。在 216 个 episode 中，后验检查竞态使仅依赖验证器的门控失效，事务性部分防护仅能阻止被覆盖的故障，而完全原子防护未记录到不安全效应；幂等请求标识可防止响应丢失后的重复效应。

Conclusion: 结果支持在智能体提交路径中显式引入证据血缘、成本感知的验证计划选择以及提交时（commit-time）强制约束，同时揭示了近似标定的局限与本地工具泛化的不足。

Abstract: Agentic systems commit state-changing actions, but additional verifiers can inherit the same upstream fault. We present VP-CONTROL, a runtime-assurance design and deterministic benchmark for cost-aware commit gates. Its 48 task templates yield 2,880 scenarios across six fault regimes. A fixed-call 2 x 2 experiment separates verifier-model diversity from evidence-source diversity. On frozen proposals from two local actor families, a cross-model vote over shared evidence approves 62.9% of unsafe proposals, versus 22.9% with an independent source. The source effect is 40.9 percentage points, compared with 11.3 for model diversity. A portfolio controller selects verification plans using only deployment-observable metadata. Approximate cluster-adjusted calibration at a nominal 5% per-task target yields 1.9% unsafe execution and 38.2% automated safe coverage on the locked test. Matched-budget portfolios also improve on fixed verification policies. Transfer remains conditional: unseen fault families yield 16-26% risk, and a FinQA check fails to reproduce the source effect with the tested small verifiers. A preregistered live HTTP/SQLite study tests concurrent writes and lost responses. After-check races defeat verifier-only gates; transactional partial guards prevent only covered failures, while a full atomic guard records no unsafe effects across 216 episodes. Idempotent request identifiers prevent duplicate effects after lost responses. The results motivate explicit evidence lineage, cost-aware selection, and commit-time enforcement, while exposing the limits of approximate calibration and local-tool generalization.

</details>


### [89] [RCL: A Retrieval-Confidence Layer for Detecting Insufficient Context in Enterprise Retrieval-Augmented Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11023)
*Chandra Mohan Ravuri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 RCL（Retrieval-Confidence Layer），一种插入检索与生成之间的轻量模块，通过结合调用图导出的结构覆盖度分数与新颖度分数，在生成开始前检测私有代码查询的检索充分性，并在置信度低于阈值时触发定向补充检索或标注人工复核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 代码生成研究多基于公开仓库，模型参数化知识可弥补检索不足；但在企业私有代码库中，私有 API、内部框架与非文档化约定完全处于预训练分布之外。已有工作表明即使完美检索也无法消除错误（失败位于下游 API 使用），而基于模型内部置信度的门控检索仅用于开放域问答。尚无工作关注生成开始前检索本身是否在结构上充分。

Method: RCL 位于检索与生成之间，包含两个评分函数：基于调用图导出的结构覆盖度分数，以及估计查询对模型先验之外知识依赖程度的新颖度分数；二者结合得到置信度，经校准阈值判定后触发定向补充检索或将输出标记为人工复核，而非在上下文不完整时静默生成。评估采用将合成内部 API 注入开源 Java 仓库构建的私有代码基准，模拟企业条件而无需专有代码。

Result: 论文报告了在第 7 节中将 RCL 与仅基于相似度的检索在生成正确性上进行比较的结果（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 作者的核心立场是：以结构化方式（而非模型内部置信度）评估的检索充分性，是构建私有企业场景下更安全代码生成系统的一个独立且当前关注不足的信号。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) for code generation has been studied extensively on public repositories, where a model's parametric knowledge often compensates for imperfect retrieval. This breaks down in enterprise codebases, where private APIs, internal frameworks, and undocumented team conventions fall entirely outside any model's pretraining distribution. Recent work on private-library code generation shows that even oracle (perfect) retrieval does not eliminate errors, but locates failures downstream in API usage; separately, confidence-gated retrieval has been studied for open-domain question answering using model-internal confidence. Neither addresses whether retrieval itself was structurally sufficient for a private-code query before generation begins. We introduce RCL (Retrieval-Confidence Layer), a lightweight module inserted between retrieval and generation that combines a call-graph-derived structural coverage score with a novelty score estimating a query's dependence on knowledge outside the model's prior, to detect insufficient retrieval before generation occurs. When confidence falls below a calibrated threshold, RCL triggers a targeted follow-up retrieval or labels the output for human review, rather than generating silently against incomplete context. We describe RCL's architecture, formalize its scoring functions, and propose an evaluation methodology using a private-code benchmark built by injecting synthetic internal APIs into open-source Java repositories, simulating the enterprise condition without proprietary code. We report results (Section 7) comparing RCL against similarity-only retrieval on generation correctness. Our position is that retrieval sufficiency, assessed structurally rather than from model-internal confidence, is a distinct and currently underaddressed signal for building safer code-generation systems in private, enterprise settings.

</details>


### [90] [SaltBench: A Referee-Gated Protocol for Measuring Method Effects in Machine-Checked Software Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11076)
*Jason Hickey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SaltBench 提出一套可测量的"机器裁判"效应评估协议，通过隔离、冻结授权与预注册预测保证结果不可事后叙事；在以 Rust/Verus 实现、以隐藏测试套件为裁判的五个系统组件上，四组受试臂中"被要求先写规约并验证"的臂在全部五个组件上代价更高，但溢价上限（2.8879×）仅属该样本总体，且随组件规模上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题：机器裁判（证明内核、程序验证器或隐藏测试套件）如何改变编码智能体的工作方式。裁判单方面判定智能体成果的价值且不可争辩，因此需要一套使裁判效应"可测量"、且结论无法被事后叙事所修饰的基准协议。

Method: 协议层面：所有结果在智能体自身工具链之外裁决；智能体被网络、参考解与测试框架隔离，并在任何计分运行前用探测手段尝试突破该隔离，使隔离成为"被观测"而非"被假定"；每次运行须经带日期的冻结授权且预测预先登记；预算中止即中止、不记为失败。受试对象为"seat"（标准 harness 中的智能体会话）。实验层面：五个系统组件全部用 Rust 编写并固定 Verus 工具链，以隐藏测试套件为裁判；设四条臂——纯智能体臂、被要求同时编写规约并验证代码的臂（方法的简化呈现，已登记）、以及两条规约先验给定并经受带日期修订扩展至 k=4 的臂。

Result: 登记符号检验未达显著性判定（4 中 3，p = 0.3125），所有溢价均低于可分辨下限。被要求"写规约并验证"的臂在全部五个组件上代价更高，具有实际量级差距：在两种已声明集合解读下，五个组件中无一溢价超过 2.8879×，其中三个最便宜组件低于 1.4×。溢价随组件规模上升，在最小组件上接近 1。

Conclusion: 该溢价上界是本样本总体的属性，而非对更大总体的承诺；验证式工作流的额外代价在小规模组件上近乎消失、随规模增长。作者公开完整记录，以保证结果的可审计性与不可事后叙事性。

Abstract: SaltBench is a benchmark protocol for one question: How does a machine referee change the way a coding agent works? A machine referee --- a proof kernel, a program verifier, or a withheld test suite --- decides what an agent's work is worth, and the agent cannot argue with it. Here we report a protocol that makes the referee's effect measurable and whose answers cannot be narrated afterwards: every outcome is decided outside the agent's own toolchain; the agent is walled off from the network, the reference solutions and the harness itself, and the wall is tested by probes that try to breach it before any scored run, so the isolation is observed rather than assumed; every run is authorized by a dated freeze with its predictions registered; and a budget stop is a halt, never a failure. In this study, the subject of the benchmark is a ``seat'', meaning an agent session in its standard harness. We tested five systems components, all authored in Rust under a pinned Verus toolchain, with a withheld test suite as the referee for each. Four arms are tested: a plain agent; an agent that is also instructed to create a specification and verify the code against it, in a reduced rendering of the method, as registered; and two arms where the specification is provided a priori, extended under a dated amendment to $k=4$, where the registered sign test reached no verdict (3 of 4, $p = 0.3125$, every premium below the resolvable floor). We found that the arm instructed to specify and verify cost more on all five components, and by a practical margin: across these five components no premium exceeded $2.8879\times$ under either reading of the declared set, and the three cheapest sat below $1.4\times$. That bound is a property of this population and not a promise about larger ones: the premium runs near $1$ on the smallest components and rises with size. We publish the complete record.

</details>


### [91] [A Model-Centric DevOps Architecture for DEVS-Based Digital Twin Simulation Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11122)
*Arnis Lektauers,Gusts Linkevičs,Guntis Mosāns,Arina Fokina,Rasa Gulbe*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出面向DEVS数字孪生的模型中心化DevOps架构，将仿真模型作为一等制品，通过声明式YAML与multiPDEVS形式映射，实现CI/CD验证、不可变版本化与Kubernetes微服务部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生仿真模型像软件一样持续演化与重新部署，但基于DEVS的引擎虽具备形式化基础，却缺乏版本管理、自动化验证与云原生持续交付支持，导致模型生命周期管理多为临时性做法。

Method: 将仿真模型视为一等DevOps制品，用声明式YAML定义并给出到multiPDEVS的形式化映射；在CI/CD流水线中执行结构与语义验证，产出不可变版本化制品，回滚即固定版本标识符；平台以Kubernetes容器化微服务分解，并对引擎做状态外化与生命周期控制适配。

Result: 以里加22路公交走廊为初始案例（里加全市多模态交通数字孪生的首次实例化），跑通完整生命周期，并报告约47,870个DEVS原子组件场景下的单容器引擎吞吐量；流水线级捕获统计与集群级多场景并发执行留待配套实证研究。

Conclusion: 该工作为DEVS数字孪生提供了模型中心化的云原生DevOps路径，使版本化、验证与持续交付内建于部署流程，为城市级交通数字孪生的可复现运维奠定基础。

Abstract: Digital twin simulation models are evolved and redeployed like software, yet DEVS-based engines offer a sound formal basis with little support for versioning, automated validation, or continuous delivery in cloud-native environments, leaving model lifecycle management ad hoc in most deployments. This paper proposes a model-centric DevOps architecture for deploying DEVS-based digital twin simulations as managed services. Simulation models are treated as first-class DevOps artefacts defined in a declarative YAML language with a formal mapping to multiPDEVS, supporting structural and semantic validation in a CI/CD pipeline that produces immutable versioned artefacts, so that reverting to an earlier validated version reduces to pinning its identifier. The platform is decomposed into containerised microservices on Kubernetes, with engine adaptations for state externalisation and lifecycle control. An initial case study on the Riga Route 22 public-transit corridor, the first instantiation of a planned city-wide multi-modal transport digital twin for Riga, Latvia, exercises the full lifecycle and reports single-container engine throughput for a scenario with roughly 47,870 DEVS atomic components; pipeline-level catch statistics and cluster-level concurrent multi-scenario execution are the subject of companion empirical studies.

</details>


### [92] [FST Pay: Deterministic Safety-Gated Architecture for Youth Digital Payments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11195)
*Shaikh Mohammed Burhan,Syed Farhaan Quadri,Tabassum Nahid Sultana*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 FST Pay——一种面向青少年实时支付的架构与形式化规范：在授权路径上采用严格确定性安全门控，AI 仅在下游生成解释，从而实现可审计、非概率化的支付安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字支付基础设施使青少年可直接使用实时金融服务，早期接入虽促进金融素养与数字包容，但也带来冲动消费、社会工程诈骗、未授权交易和商户剥削等风险。传统对策依赖概率性机器学习或刚性静态控制，而让概率性或生成式 AI 直接影响实时支付授权会引入非确定性、不可预测的边界行为与关键审计漏洞。

Method: 将 FST Pay 构建为架构与形式化规范，基于不可变的操作边界：实时授权路径上实施严格确定性安全门控，并与下游 AI 解释解耦。对经 UPI 等渠道发起的交易执行六项确定性不变量检查：消费限额、监护人共同签署策略、交易金额阈值、商户类别代码、时间访问区间、硬件完整性约束。通过有序、互斥的决策函数将交易严格分类为 ALLOW、REVIEW 或 BLOCK；高风险交易触发异步监护人共同签署工作流；生成式 AI 被完全置于结算下游，仅消费已发布的决策后事件以生成自然语言财务洞察，且对账本无修改权限。

Result: 通过六项确定性不变量检查与三值互斥决策函数，实现了授权决策的确定性与可审计性；异步监护人共同签署工作流处理高风险交易；生成式 AI 在结算后仅生成解释性洞察，不干预账本状态。

Conclusion: FST Pay 以"确定性安全门控 + 解耦下游 AI 解释"的架构边界，避免了概率性/生成式 AI 直接干预实时支付授权所带来的非确定性与审计风险，为青少年实时支付提供了一种可形式化验证的安全框架。

Abstract: Digital payment infrastructures increasingly provide adolescent users with direct access to real-time financial services. While early access promotes financial literacy and digital inclusion, it exposes young users to severe risks of impulsive spending, social engineering frauds, unauthorized transactions, and merchant exploitation. Conventional countermeasures rely on probabilistic machine learning or rigid static controls. However, allowing probabilistic or generative artificial intelligence (AI) models to directly influence real-time payment authorization introduces non-determinism, unpredictable edge-case behavior, and critical audit vulnerabilities. This paper introduces Financial Safety for Teens Pay (FST Pay) as an architectural and formal specification. FST Pay is founded on an immutable operational boundary: strict deterministic safety gating on the real-time authorization path coupled with decoupled downstream AI explanation. Transactions initiated via rails like UPI are subjected to six deterministic invariant checks covering spending limits, guardian co-sign policies, transaction amount thresholds, merchant category codes, temporal access intervals, and hardware integrity constraints. Transactions are classified strictly into ALLOW, REVIEW, or BLOCK outcomes through an ordered, mutually exclusive decision function. High-risk transactions trigger an asynchronous guardian co-sign workflow. Generative AI is relegated entirely downstream of settlement, consuming published post-decision events solely to generate natural-language financial insights without holding mutation privileges over the ledger.

</details>


### [93] [TripleBound: Triplet-Guided Heterogeneous Graph Learning for Microservice Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11212)
*Mineth Weerasinghe,Himindu Kularathne,Methmini Madhushika,Danuka Lakshan,Nisansa de Silva,Adeesha Wijayasiri,Srinath Perera*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 TripleBound，一种将异构图神经网络与弱监督三元组约束结合的混合框架，用于单体系统到微服务的自动化分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单体到微服务迁移因紧耦合与服务边界不清晰而困难；现有分解方法通常只依赖结构依赖或语义相似度中的一种信号，很少在统一表示学习目标中同时整合两者。

Method: 在异构图神经网络中引入由解析器推断的服务分组（基于包结构、命名约定、代码位置）生成的弱监督三元组约束，并将三元组约束注入共享的结构潜空间，使两类信号联合优化。结构依赖由 CHGNN 建模，将单体表示为含程序节点、资源节点、CALL 边与 CRUD 边的异构图；语义关系通过三元组约束引入。

Result: 在 AcmeAir、DayTrader、PlantsByWebSphere、JPetStore 上评估：在所选权重下，TripleBound 在 AcmeAir、DayTrader、JPetStore 上取得最高综合分解得分，优于 CHGNN 与 MonoEmbed，但在 PlantsByWebSphere 上 CHGNN 更优。分项指标显示存在权衡：结构模块度与分区间耦合改善的同时，部分数据集上实体分布不均衡度上升。更换综合权重后，TripleBound 在 AcmeAir 和 DayTrader 仍保持第一，但 JPetStore 上不再如此，说明综合排名依赖具体指标。

Conclusion: TripleBound 通过统一潜空间联合优化结构依赖与语义信号，可提升多数据集上的综合分解效果，但其优势具有数据集与权重依赖性，且存在模块度、耦合与分布均衡之间的取舍。

Abstract: Cloud computing and DevOps have made microservices a common architecture for scalable, maintainable software systems. However, migrating monoliths to microservices remains challenging due to tight coupling and unclear service boundaries. Existing decomposition approaches typically rely on either structural dependencies or semantic similarity signals, but rarely integrate both within a unified representation learning objective. This paper proposes TripleBound, a hybrid framework for automated monolith-to-microservices decomposition that augments a heterogeneous graph neural network with weakly supervised triplet constraints derived from parser-inferred service groups based on package structure, naming conventions, and code location. TripleBound injects triplet-based constraints directly into the shared structural latent space, enabling both signals to be jointly optimized during representation learning. Structural dependencies are captured using CHGNN, which models the monolith as a heterogeneous graph with program nodes, resource nodes, CALL edges, and CRUD edges. Semantic relationships are incorporated through triplet constraints generated from parser-inferred service groups. Evaluation on AcmeAir, DayTrader, PlantsByWebSphere, and JPetStore shows that TripleBound achieves the highest composite decomposition score under the selected weighting on AcmeAir, DayTrader, and JPetStore compared to CHGNN and MonoEmbed, while CHGNN remains stronger on PlantsByWebSphere. Per-metric analysis reveals trade-offs: gains in structural modularity and inter-partition coupling are accompanied by higher entity distribution imbalance on some datasets. Alternative composite weightings preserve TripleBound's first-place ranking on AcmeAir and DayTrader but not on JPetStore, showing that the aggregate ranking is metric-dependent.

</details>


### [94] [Exploring the Role of Security Experience and ChatGPT Usage Strategies on Secure Software Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11303)
*Alessio Ferrari,Minh An Nguyen,Kushal Ramkumar,Liliana Pasquale*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项针对26名网络安全硕士生的探索性研究发现，学生使用ChatGPT的策略与其网络安全专业水平无关；单一使用模式与成绩无显著关联，但使用模式的多样性（采用的不同策略数量）在控制专业水平后仍与作业表现正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正快速进入软件工程教育，但其在"安全软件工程教育"中的作用研究不足；作者希望刻画学生在漏洞修复任务中使用ChatGPT的方式，并检验使用模式与既有网络安全专业知识是否与作业成绩相关。

Method: 对某非全日制网络安全硕士项目中26名研究生在漏洞修复作业期间使用ChatGPT的交互日志进行探索性实证研究；采用结构化双人编码（double-coding）程序对日志进行归类，以识别使用模式；随后分析使用模式、使用多样性与既有网络安全专业知识同作业成绩之间的关联。

Result: 不同网络安全专业水平的学生采用了大致相似的ChatGPT策略；各单项使用模式按成绩呈现描述性差异，但在多重比较校正后无一具有统计显著性。相反，ChatGPT使用多样性（采用的不同使用模式数量）与表现正相关，且在控制网络安全专业知识后依然成立。

Conclusion: 学生"如何使用"ChatGPT可能比"是否使用"更具信息价值；这些探索性发现为后续开展对照研究、引导学生在安全软件工程教育中有效使用LLM提供了动机。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) is reshaping software engineering education, but their role in secure software engineering education remains underexplored. We report an exploratory empirical study of how 26 graduate students in a part-time MSc Cybersecurity programme used ChatGPT during a vulnerability-fixing assignment. To characterise ChatGPT use, we analysed students' ChatGPT interaction logs using a structured double-coding procedure and examined whether usage patterns and prior cybersecurity expertise were associated with assignment performance. The results show that students with varying levels of cybersecurity expertise used broadly similar ChatGPT strategies. Individual usage patterns showed descriptive differences by grade, but none remained statistically significant after correcting for multiple comparisons. In contrast, diversity of ChatGPT usage, i.e., the number of distinct usage patterns adopted, was positively associated with performance, even after controlling for cybersecurity expertise. These exploratory findings suggest that the way students engage with ChatGPT may be more informative than whether they use it, and motivate future controlled studies to guide students toward effective LLM use in secure software engineering education.

</details>


### [95] [CoSTAR: Data Synthesis-Driven Constraint-Aware COBOL Section Summarization for Legacy System Modernization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11332)
*Hao Lin,He Jiang,Xiaochen Li,Weihong Sun,Yufu Wang,Zhilei Ren,Ang Jia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CoSTAR 通过执行验证的数据合成与约束感知训练，使可本地部署的小型 LLM 在 COBOL 段落级代码摘要任务上媲美超大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COBOL 仍广泛应用于政府、金融机构和大型企业，但技术老化、专家减少与文档缺失使遗留系统现代化日益紧迫。代码摘要是支撑遗留系统理解的前置环节，而 COBOL 段落级摘要面临两大挑战：数据稀缺，以及迁移约束的保持。

Method: 提出 CoSTAR 集成框架，将执行验证的数据合成与约束感知模型训练相结合。其复用通用编程任务，通过 LLM 生成合成执行验证的 COBOL 代码-摘要数据以缓解数据稀缺；在此数据基础上，为目标段落补充相关数据声明与自然语言解释，并利用约束引导的结构化推理依据训练较小的基础 LLM，使其保持 COBOL 段落摘要所需的迁移约束。

Result: 在公开与企业机密 COBOL 系统上评估：合成 3,764 条执行验证训练实例；基于 7B/8B 基础 LLM 的 CoSTAR 平均相对提升 ROUGE-L 25.38%、METEOR 53.84%、chrF 37.22%；真实企业场景中，仅基于 Qwen3-8B 的 CoSTAR 在准确性、完整性和简洁性上优于企业部署的 Qwen3-235B。

Conclusion: CoSTAR 使小型、可本地部署的 LLM 在隐私敏感的 COBOL 遗留系统上达到与远大规模 LLM 相当的性能，为 COBOL 代码摘要与遗留系统现代化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: COBOL remains critical to governments, financial institutions, and large enterprises; yet, aging technologies, shrinking expertise, and missing documentation make modernization of COBOL-based legacy systems increasingly urgent. Before migration, code summarization is a common practice to support legacy system understanding. However, COBOL code summarization, especially on section-level, faces two key challenges: data scarcity and migration constraint preservation. To address these challenges, we propose CoSTAR, an integrated framework that combines execution-validated data synthesis with constraint-aware model training. CoSTAR repurposes general-purpose programming tasks to synthesize execution-validated COBOL code-summary data through LLM-based generation to overcome data scarcity. Based on the synthesized data, CoSTAR augments target sections with relevant data declarations and natural-language explanations, and uses constraint-guided structured rationales to train smaller base LLMs. The trained LLMs preserve the migration constraints for COBOL section summarization. We evaluate CoSTAR on both public and confidential enterprise COBOL systems. CoSTAR effectively synthesizes 3,764 execution-validated training instances. Based on these instances, CoSTAR built on 7B/8B base LLMs can improve these LLMs with average relative gains of 25.38% on ROUGE-L, 53.84% on METEOR, and 37.22% on chrF.
