In real-world enterprise evaluation, CoSTAR built on only Qwen3-8B, outperforms the enterprise-deployed Qwen3-235B in accuracy, completeness, and conciseness. These results show that CoSTAR enables small, locally deployable LLMs to achieve performance competitive with substantially larger LLMs for privacy-sensitive COBOL legacy systems.

</details>


### [96] [Agent-Integrated Software: Interaction Contracts and Continuous Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11381)
*Shengcheng Yu,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出将智能体集成视为软件工程问题，引入"智能体集成软件"(AIS)模式与"意图级交互抽象"(IIA)，通过开放转移系统模型、交互契约与持续保证机制，把任务级交互与应用行为建立显式对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在既有应用中嵌入智能体后，用户可随时修改目标、操作共享对象，而委托执行仍在进行，导致持久的协调问题。局部组件正确并不能保证整体可信，需要显式对应任务级交互与应用行为。

Method: 提出AIS软件模式（常规内核+直接交互+内置智能体）与IIA任务语义；用开放转移系统模型刻画AIS执行与IIA状态/事件的关系；以交互契约（任务绑定、角色权限、控制转移、结果证据）约束该关系；持续保证机制在依赖变化时维护限定范围内的声明；并用紧凑披露契约与条件命题进行说明。

Result: 论证了局部组件有效性不足的原因，展示了如何将选定的准入不变式与规划过程分离；通过跨软件领域的对比揭示了框架的假设与局限。

Conclusion: 该研究展望提出涵盖应用抽象、开发支持、受控执行、质量评估与人类监督的研究议程，目标是把智能体集成发展为可维护的软件工程学科。

Abstract: Embedding an intelligent agent in an existing application creates a persistent coordination problem: users can revise goals and manipulate shared objects while delegated execution continues. We argue that dependable integration requires an explicit correspondence between task-level interaction and application behavior. We introduce Agent-Integrated Software (AIS) as a software pattern combining a conventional core, direct interaction, and a built-in agent, and Intent-Level Interaction Abstraction (IIA) as the task semantics through which users inspect and control delegated work. An open transition-system model relates AIS execution to IIA states and events. Interaction contracts constrain this relation through task bindings, role-specific authority, control transitions, and outcome evidence; continuous assurance maintains scoped claims as their dependencies change. A compact disclosure contract and conditional propositions illustrate why local component validity is insufficient and how selected admission invariants can be separated from planning. Contrasting software domains expose the framework's assumptions and limits. This perspective develops a research agenda spanning application abstraction, development support, controlled execution, quality assessment, and human supervision, with the aim of making agent integration a maintainable software engineering discipline.

</details>


### [97] [Deep Learning-based Bug Triage System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11420)
*Sourabh Pal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出基于RoBERTa-base的自动化缺陷分类系统，5个训练轮次内达到0.90的缺陷识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效的缺陷分诊对简化软件开发流程至关重要，需准确分类并指派所报缺陷，而人工分诊效率低、易出错，因此需要自动化方案。

Method: 基于预训练RoBERTa-base transformer架构构建自动化缺陷分诊系统，利用深层上下文表示对缺陷报告进行分类，以优化分派流程，并采用微调方式进行训练。

Result: 实验评估显示，所提系统在仅5个训练轮次内即取得0.90的缺陷识别准确率。

Conclusion: 结果印证了微调transformer模型在实际软件工程自动化中的高效性与高性能，适合作为缺陷分诊的实用方案。

Abstract: Effective bug triage is crucial for streamlining the software development lifecycle by accurately categorizing and assigning reported software defects. In this paper, we propose an automated bug triage system built upon the pre-trained RoBERTa-base transformer architecture. By leveraging deep contextual representations, our approach efficiently classifies incoming bug reports to optimize assignment. Experimental evaluation demonstrates that the proposed system achieves a strong bug identification accuracy of 0.90 within just five training epochs. These findings highlight the efficiency and high performance of fine-tuned transformer models for practical software engineering automation.

</details>


### [98] [ChurnBench: A Drift-Aware Benchmark Demonstrating That Refresh Scheduling, Not Cache Age, Governs Staleness in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11515)
*Vivek Kumar Singh,Preeti Priyam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出开源基准 ChurnBench，以时间线方式生成四源企业数据织物，并借助仅追加的真值账本区分“新鲜度错误”与“推理错误”，发现调度刷新下缓存年龄不能预测过期程度，真正的变量是 TTL 配置与实体变化率的匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索基准将数据冻结为快照，只能检验智能体是否检索到正确段落，无法判断其答案在数据持续变更（许可重分配、用户离职、价格变动、合同续签）的现实场景下是否仍然成立，因此缺少对“答案时效性”的评估手段。

Method: 构建 ChurnBench 基准：生成由四个数据源组成的企业数据织物，并以时间线而非快照形式组织；所有变更写入仅追加的真值账本，金标准答案仅由账本计算而非取自在线存储。对每个案例在检索时刻与评估时刻分别解析真值，从而识别检索时正确、评估时错误的“新鲜度错误”，并与推理错误区分。实验覆盖缓存年龄 1、14、28 天，并做关闭分层刷新的对照消融。

Result: 在缓存年龄 1、14、28 天下，新鲜度错误数分别为 7、4、4，说明调度式刷新以 TTL 界定过期上限，且各窗口内均未观察到 TTL 失效，缓存年龄不能预测过期程度。消融实验中，关闭分层刷新使 28 天下新鲜度错误从 4 升至 45，而 1 天下保持不变，验证了机制归因。

Conclusion: 漂移类基准应扫描的变量是 TTL 配置与各实体变化率的匹配关系，而非漂移窗口长度。ChurnBench、评测框架及全部逐错误数据均开源发布。

Abstract: In production, agentic systems answer questions over data that lives in several places and keeps changing: licenses are reassigned, users offboarded, prices changed, contracts renewed. Existing retrieval benchmarks freeze the data, so they cannot ask whether an agent's answer is still true, only whether it found the right passage. We present ChurnBench, an open-source benchmark that generates a four-source enterprise data fabric as a timeline rather than a snapshot. Every change is written to an append-only ground-truth ledger, and gold answers are computed from that ledger, never from the live stores. An answer that was correct when its data was retrieved but wrong when evaluated is therefore detected and labeled a freshness error, distinct from a reasoning error; we validate this by resolving ground truth at both timestamps for every case reported. Using the instrument, we find that when a system refreshes on a schedule, cache age does not predict staleness. Across cache ages of 1, 14, and 28 days, freshness errors were 7, 4, and 4, because scheduled refresh bounds staleness by time-to-live, and no TTL lapse was observed in any window. A controlled ablation confirms the mechanism: disabling tiered refresh raises freshness errors from 4 to 45 at 28 days and leaves them identical at one day. The variable a drift benchmark should sweep is therefore TTL configuration against each entity's rate of change, not drift-window length. ChurnBench, the evaluation harness, and all per-error data are released open source.

</details>


### [99] [PRISMA-LLM: An Empirical Reporting Framework for AI-Assisted Systematic Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11559)
*Miguel Zabaleta,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文基于888篇综述自动化论文（14,726条标注）的SciLitBench语料库，刻画了LLM与软件类工作流在方法、综述阶段、评估与局限报告上的变化，并据此提出PRISMA-LLM框架，将实现披露与后果敏感型评估及局限报告相分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM与AI软件日益参与系统综述决策，但审计这些工作流所需信息的报告方式不一致，缺乏可审计性与可比性。

Method: 构建并分析SciLitBench语料库（888篇综述自动化论文、14,726条标注），统计方法、综述阶段使用、评估方式与局限报告的变化趋势。

Result: 自动化转向面向LLM与软件的工作流，涉及时可改变证据基的综述阶段；2023年以来，38.0%的软件/产品类论文未报告任何评估，而LLM类论文为9.3%；报告覆盖率随LLM工作流复杂度上升，但52%的仅报告正面结果的LLM评估仍存在未满足的可靠性或性能要求。

Conclusion: 据此提出PRISMA-LLM框架，以实证为基础，将实现披露与后果敏感型评估及局限报告分离开来。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and AI-enabled software increasingly participate in systematic-review decisions, yet the information needed to audit these workflows is reported inconsistently. We analyze SciLitBench, a corpus of 888 review-automation papers with 14,726 annotations, to characterize changes in methods, review-stage use, evaluation and reported limitations. Automation has shifted toward LLM- and software-facing workflows, including stages that can alter the evidence base. Since 2023, 38.0% of software/product papers reported no evaluation, compared with 9.3% of LLM papers. Reporting coverage increased with LLM workflow complexity, yet 52% of positive-only LLM evaluations still reported an unmet reliability or performance requirement. From these patterns, we introduce PRISMA-LLM, an empirically grounded framework separating implementation disclosure from consequence-sensitive evaluation and limitation reporting.

</details>


### [100] [Ecdysis: Efficient and Effective Training of Runtime Harnesses for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11677)
*Ruiqing Yue,Yu Cui,Zhuoyu Sun,Sicheng Pan,Xianhong Xue,Tingyu Li,Ting Li,Wenzhuo Zhu,Yi Chen,Yifei Liu,Baohan Huang,Zhe Cui,Haibin Zhang,Cong Zuo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 Ecdysis 框架，通过跨实例失败聚合与多角色协同诊断，区分模型特有问题与 harness 系统性缺陷，实现更高效、更泛化的 harness 自演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 harness 演化方法依赖迭代搜索，反复评估与修改候选 harness，导致执行与改码的时间开销大，且易过拟合已观测任务与特定失败模式，泛化性下降。作者指出核心瓶颈在于缺乏原则性的失败诊断：单个失败既可能源于模型自身缺陷，也可能源于 harness 的系统性缺陷，直接针对个体失败优化会引入不必要的模型特定适配。

Method: Ecdysis 将"模型特定适配"与"harness 层级修复"加以区分，使适配偏向系统性 harness 缺陷。方法包含两点：一是采用批次级跨实例失败聚合范式，联合分析多个任务实例的失败证据，以识别跨任务复现的失败模式；二是引入 Failure-Driven Collaborative Refinement，通过多角色协作诊断失败原因，并迭代细化 harness 修改规范。

Result: 实验表明，Ecdysis 相比现有 harness 演化方法在 harness 训练上最高取得 1.84 倍加速，同时所得 harness 的推理准确率提升 18.56%。

Conclusion: 以原则性失败诊断为切入点，用跨实例聚合与多角色协同细化替代逐一失败的迭代搜索，可在更低训练开销下获得更强、更可泛化的 harness，为 LLM 智能体运行时 harness 的自演化提供了高效范式。

Abstract: Self-evolving runtime harnesses can substantially improve the capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents and provide a promising paradigm for optimizing agent execution. Existing harness evolution methods typically rely on iterative search, repeatedly evaluating and revising candidate harnesses based on execution feedback from task instances. While this paradigm enables continuous harness optimization, it incurs substantial time overhead due to repeated agent executions and code modifications, and may overfit to observed tasks and specific failure patterns, resulting in degraded generalization to unseen tasks. We identify the lack of principled failure diagnosis as a key bottleneck in harness evolution: an observed failure can reflect either model-specific deficiencies or systematic harness deficiencies, and directly optimizing against individual failures can lead to unnecessary model-specific accommodation. We therefore propose Ecdysis, an efficient and effective framework that distinguishes model-specific accommodation from harness-level repair and biases adaptation toward systematic harness deficiencies by identifying recurring cross-task failure patterns. Ecdysis adopts a batch-level cross-instance failure aggregation paradigm to jointly analyze failure evidence from multiple task instances and further introduces Failure-Driven Collaborative Refinement to diagnose failure causes and iteratively refine harness modification specifications. By combining cross-instance failure analysis with multi-role diagnosis, Ecdysis enables more effective harness evolution with lower training time. Experiments show that Ecdysis achieves up to a 1.84x speedup in harness training compared with existing harness evolution methods, while improving the reasoning accuracy of the resulting harnesses by 18.56%.

</details>


### [101] [Reproducibility in the Age of Agentic AI: Context Engineering at the Timescale of a Codebase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11728)
*Lorena A. Barba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AI编码智能体可降低维护测试、提交历史、仓库结构、指令与决策记录等可复现性实践的成本，并使其收益即时化；但研究者仍需对产物及其编码的科学判断负责。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复现性研究实践通常被视为额外负担，维护成本高、收益滞后，研究者缺乏持续投入的动力。作者试图重新定位这些实践的角色，论证其作为"上下文工程"对AI编码智能体具有直接价值，从而改变成本—收益结构。

Method: 采用概念性论证（position/argument 类论文）：将可复现性实践重新概念化为面向AI编码智能体的"上下文工程"（context engineering），从成本降低与收益即时化两方面进行理论辨析，并界定研究者不可转移的责任边界。

Result: 论证得出：智能体能够降低测试、提交历史、仓库结构、指令与决策记录的维护成本，同时让这些产物的收益立刻显现（直接改善智能体的工作上下文与输出质量），从而在激励层面支持可复现性实践的持续采纳。

Conclusion: 可复现性实践与AI编码智能体形成互惠关系：前者作为上下文工程提升后者效能，后者降低前者维护成本。但责任并未转移——研究者必须验证这些产物以及其中蕴含的科学判断，科学责任仍由人承担。

Abstract: Reproducible research practices are context engineering for AI coding agents. I argue that agents lower the cost of maintaining tests, commit histories, repository structure, instructions, and decision records while making their benefits immediate. Researchers remain responsible for verifying these artifacts and the scientific judgments they encode.

</details>


### [102] [An analysis of the relationship of input metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11824)
*Addison Crump*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对输入度量（input metrics）缺乏系统比较的问题，借助分区测试文献中用于其他度量类别的方法，对常见输入度量进行严格比较，并提出新度量 $k$-alt-path。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输入度量用于以测试套件中输入的覆盖特征来评估测试进展。尽管自20世纪50年代以来已提出多种此类度量，但少有研究对其进行比较，导致度量选择与改进缺乏依据。

Method: 首先定义并回顾常见输入度量；通过一个简短案例研究说明典型经验比较策略不足以比较度量；随后定义并实现 $k$-alt-path，以展示如何严格地改进标准度量；最后系统比较其余常见输入度量并讨论发现的意义。

Result: 案例研究表明，典型的经验比较策略从根本上不足以比较度量；$k$-alt-path 相比 $k$-path 在降低冗余的同时提升了敏感性；其余常见输入度量得到系统比较。

Conclusion: 本文提出了分区测试分析方法，为输入度量的合理化改进提供了依据，并形成了未来研究策略，以持续精化输入度量。

Abstract: Input metrics evaluate the progress of testing in terms of features of inputs present in a test suite. Previous works, as early as the 1950s, established a number of such metrics, but few endeavored to compare them. This paper does so by utilizing existing methods proposed for other metric classes in partition testing literature. After defining and reviewing common input metrics, we begin with a short case study revealing that typical empirical comparison strategies are fundamentally insufficient for comparing metrics. Then, we demonstrate how one rigorously improves a standard metric by defining and implementing $k$-alt-path, a new metric which reduces redundancy while improving sensitivity over $k$-path. Each of the other common input metrics are then systematically compared before discussing the implications of our findings. With these contributions, we bring forward partition testing analysis methods that justify and form a strategy for future research in refining input metrics.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [When Synthetic Data Hurts: On Catastrophic Forgetting in Skill Retrieval for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10750)
*Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Yifan Nie,Utkarsh Soni,Eugene Wen,Arvid Frydenlund*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文面向 LLM 智能体的技能路由问题，在 34,396 个技能库上构建生产级技能路由器，并系统研究有限真实监督与合成数据下的技能检索。发现合成数据微调虽提升分布内检索，却导致对真实与 OOD 数据的灾难性遗忘；引入多种持续学习式缓解方法后，既保留 OOD 检索性能，又将合成分布内检索提升 13.98%，为稀缺多正例监督提供实用基准与稳健微调方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体日益依赖运行时检索外部技能，从大规模技能库中准确选择技能成为关键挑战。同时，真实监督标注稀缺，常需借助合成数据微调，但合成数据可能带来灾难性遗忘，损害真实与 OOD 场景的检索能力，因此需要研究在此约束下的稳健技能检索与微调方法。

Method: 构建覆盖 34,396 个技能的生产级技能路由器，并在有限真实监督结合合成数据的设定下开展大规模技能检索研究。采用合成数据微调检索器/重排器，随后引入受持续学习启发的多种遗忘缓解微调方法：嵌入锚点正则化、Learning without Forgetting（LwF）、Elastic Weight Consolidation（EWC）以及 L2 初始化。

Result: 合成数据微调提升了分布内检索，但在真实数据与分布外（OOD）数据上造成灾难性遗忘。上述缓解方法不仅保留了 OOD 技能检索性能，还在 0.6B Qwen 检索器与重排器上将合成分布内技能检索提升 13.98%。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了面向稀缺、多正例监督场景的实用基准与稳健微调配方，表明持续学习式正则与初始化策略可有效缓解合成数据微调带来的灾难性遗忘，并在 OOD 泛化与分布内性能之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external skills retrieved at runtime, making skill selection from large repositories a critical challenge. We present a production skill router over 34,396 skills and a large-scale study of skill retrieval using limited real supervision and synthetic data. We found that the synthetic-data fine-tuning improves in-distribution retrieval but it causes catastrophic forgetting on real and out-of-distribution (OOD) data. We evaluate several forgetting mitigation fine-tuning approaches inspired by continual learning, including embedding-anchor regularization, Learning without Forgetting (LwF), Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC), and L2-initialization. The results show that these approaches not only retain the performance on OOD skills retrieval but also improve the retrieval on synthetic in-distribution skills by 13.98\% for 0.6B Qwen retriever and reranker. Our results provide a practical benchmark and a robust fine-tuning recipe for scarce, multi-positive supervision.

</details>


### [104] [UniRec: Cross-stage Multi-Task Fusion with Preference Alignment for Cascaded Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11052)
*Lingyuan Kong,Jiaqi Cui,Fanjiao Zeng,Congqi Wang,Yu Li,Yuan Cheng,Jingxin Liu,Xiaoshuang Chen,Kaiqiao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 UniRec 统一跨阶段推荐融合模型，通过共享嵌入、双轴偏好对齐与属性分组正则，实现精排-粗排融合的联合优化，离线优于基线，在线时长提升0.616%并全量部署于快手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级推荐系统采用多级级联架构，各阶段目标、特征空间与延迟约束不同。若分别优化粗排与精排，会产生跨阶段不一致：上游可能过滤掉下游偏好的物品，而下游独立调参的融合又会抵消上游改进。现有多任务融合方法多局限于精排阶段的多目标融合，跨阶段方法通常仅在上游加入下游分数因子，缺乏对两阶段融合模块的联合优化。

Method: 1）两个融合代理部分共享输入嵌入并在单一计算图中训练，使任一阶段的梯度经共享表征相互影响；2）双轴偏好对齐目标：纵向跨阶段一致性项将下游成对偏好迁移至上游融合分数，横向紧致聚合项将异构先验信号上的数十个成对目标重组为双向偏好证据；3）属性分组相对正则：在属性组内计算优势并按同组归一化策略，使整体提升某个高回报属性组不产生优化收益，抑制对高回报区域的过度集中。

Result: 离线实验中，UniRec 稳定优于单阶段融合与跨阶段协同类基线；在线 A/B 测试显示 App 使用时长提升0.616%，并已在快手平台全量部署。

Conclusion: 跨阶段融合模块的联合优化可有效缓解级联推荐的一致性损失，共享表征学习、双轴偏好对齐与属性分组正则构成可行方案，并在大规模工业场景中得到验证。

Abstract: Industrial recommender systems use cascaded stages with different objectives, feature spaces, and latency constraints. Optimizing pre-ranking and ranking separately can create cross-stage inconsistency: upstream models may filter out items preferred by downstream rankers, and independently tuned downstream fusion can offset upstream improvements. Existing multi-task fusion methods focus on multi-objective fusion within the ranking stage, and cross-stage methods typically only add a downstream score factor to upstream ranking. Joint optimization of fusion modules across both stages remains largely unexplored.
  We propose UniRec, a Unified Cross-stage Recommendation Fusion model. First, the two fusion agents partially share input embeddings and are trained in a single computation graph, so gradients from either stage propagate through the shared representation and influence the other. Second, we introduce a dual-axis preference alignment objective: a vertical cross-stage consistency term transfers downstream pairwise preferences to the upstream fusion score, and a horizontal compact aggregation term reorganizes dozens of pairwise objectives over heterogeneous prior signals into bidirectional preference evidence. Third, we find that unconstrained end-to-end fusion optimization can exploit imbalances in item attribute distributions, over-concentrating on high-reward regions at the cost of other objectives. We therefore add an attribute group-relative regularization that computes advantages within attribute groups and normalizes the policy over the same groups, so uniformly promoting an entire high-reward group yields no optimization gain. Offline, UniRec consistently outperforms single-stage fusion and cross-stage coordination baselines. Online A/B tests show a 0.616\% gain in app usage duration. UniRec is fully deployed on the Kuaishou platform.

</details>


### [105] [VikingRAG: Accurate and Token-efficient Retrieval-augmented Generation over Structured Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11390)
*Peiyuan Gao,Gaoyuan Zhang,Haojie Qin,Yahui Sun,Qianyi Zhang,Yunhao Zhang,Zeyu Wang,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VikingRAG 是一个目录感知的语义数据管理系统，通过语义与结构访问的紧耦合、检索轨迹复用与自适应升级策略，在大幅降低 token 消耗的同时保持高 RAG 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 方法利用文档结构获取充分证据，但结构性上下文带来高昂的 token 成本，需要在保证高准确率的前提下降低结构与多轮交互的 token 开销。

Method: 提出目录感知的语义数据管理系统 VikingRAG，紧耦合语义与结构访问以支持结构上下文高效、证据缺口驱动的多轮检索；将智能体多轮检索轨迹物化为经验边并在相似查询中复用；引入自适应升级策略，证据充分时用单轮经验增强检索回答，否则才触发智能体多轮检索。

Result: 真实数据集实验表明，基础系统 VikingRAG 在达到最先进方法高准确率的同时仅消耗其 11.6%–51.9% 的 token；结合轨迹复用与自适应升级后，token 成本降至 5.1%–32.5%，并保持有竞争力的准确率与实用的文档存储性能。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了结构上下文高效、经验复用与自适应检索策略在新兴 AI 知识库场景中的实用价值。

Abstract: State-of-the-art retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods exploit document structures to acquire sufficient evidence, but often incur substantial token costs. To reduce structural-context tokens without compromising high RAG accuracy, we present {\sf VikingRAG}, a directory-aware semantic data management system that tightly integrates semantic and structural access to support structural-context-efficient, evidence-gap-driven multi-round retrieval. To further reduce token overhead of multi-round interaction, we materialize agentic multi-round retrieval traces as experience edges, and reuse these edges for similar queries, avoiding repeated multi-round exploration. To additionally reduce token costs when agentic multi-round retrieval is unnecessary, we introduce an adaptive escalation strategy that answers from one-round experience-augmented retrieval when the evidence is sufficient, and invokes agentic multi-round retrieval only otherwise. Experiments on real datasets show that the base system {\sf VikingRAG} matches high accuracy of state-of-the-art methods while consuming only 11.6\%--51.9\% of their tokens. With retrieval-trace reuse and adaptive escalation, token costs drop to 5.1\%--32.5\% while maintaining competitive accuracy and practical document-storage performance, showing the utility of this work for emerging AI knowledge bases.

</details>


### [106] [TimelyRAG: Semantic-Temporal Hybrid Retrieval for Time-Critical Question Answering in Overlapping-Evolving Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11572)
*Youngeun Nam,Joeun Kim,Hwanjun Song,Susik Yoon,Jae-Gil Lee,Byung Suk Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TimelyRAG，一个与检索器无关的框架，将时间距离引入排序以应对重叠演化文档，并发布TimelyQABench基准，nDCG@10最高提升28.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向大语言模型与检索增强生成的时间敏感检索方法仅针对"不相交演化"环境（每次更新为独立快照），而法律、政策、法规常处于"重叠演化"环境：修订会覆盖早期条款但保留大部分内容，导致版本间语义高度重叠。因此需要新的检索框架与评测基准。

Method: 提出TimelyRAG，一个与检索器无关的框架，将时间距离（temporal distance）纳入排序过程，使查询与版本适配的文档对齐；同时构建TimelyQABench，首个面向重叠演化挑战、以法规密集领域为主的基准。

Result: 实验显示一致的性能提升，nDCG@10最高提升+28.6%，凸显时间推理对演化文档上可靠问答的重要性。

Conclusion: 时间推理是应对重叠演化文档可靠问答的关键，TimelyRAG与TimelyQABench为相关研究提供了有效方法与评测平台，资源已开源。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) have advanced open-domain question answering (QA), they remain unreliable when documents evolve through amendments. Existing time-sensitive retrieval methods address only the disjoint-evolving environment, where each update is an independent snapshot. However, laws, policies, and regulations often operate in overlapping-evolving environments, where amendments override earlier clauses while preserving most content, creating strong semantic overlap across versions. We propose TimelyRAG, a retriever-agnostic framework that incorporates temporal distance into ranking to align queries with version-appropriate documents. We also introduce TimelyQABench, the first benchmark for regulation-heavy domains with overlapping-evolving challenges. Experiments show consistent gains, up to +28.6% in nDCG@10, highlighting the importance of temporal reasoning for reliable QA over evolving documents. All resources are available at https://github.com/kaist-dmlab/TimelyRAG.

</details>


### [107] [FedHUR: Learning Hierarchical Utility-Guided Client Relations for Personalized Federated Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11632)
*Mingzhe Han,Jiahao Liu,Dongsheng Li,Jiankui Zhou,Hansu Gu,Peng Zhang,Ning Gu,Tun Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出FedHUR框架，以item-item filters为对象构建层次化效用引导的客户端关系，用于联邦推荐中的个性化聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦推荐个性化聚合方法通常基于预定义参数假设（如参数相似性或互补性）构建单一全局客户端关系，难以刻画推荐中用户关系的层次性与多粒度特性，且这些预定义关系无法直接反映相关客户端聚合后能否提升预测性能。

Method: FedHUR以item-item filters作为关系构建与聚合的对象。首先聚合并聚类各客户端的本地信息以获得全局层次化信息；每个客户端基于本地信息与全局层次化信息计算层次化效用信号，指示哪些协作信息有助于提升自身预测；服务器利用这些效用信号检索对该客户端有用的客户端，进行进一步的个性化聚合。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，FedHUR持续优于现有联邦推荐基线，验证了层次化效用引导客户端关系学习的有效性。

Conclusion: FedHUR通过层次化效用引导的客户端关系学习，克服了预定义单一全局关系的局限，为联邦推荐中的个性化聚合提供了有效方案；代码已开源。

Abstract: Federated recommendation enables collaborative model training while keeping user interaction data on local clients. A central problem in federated recommendation is how to aggregate useful information across clients for personalized recommendation. Existing personalized aggregation methods usually construct client relations from predefined parameter-based assumptions, such as parameter similarity or complementarity, and use these relations to determine aggregation weights. However, such methods construct a single global relation, which is insufficient to capture the hierarchical and multi-granularity nature of user relations in recommendation. Moreover, these predefined relations cannot directly reflect whether the related clients can improve prediction performance after aggregation. To address these limitations, we propose FedHUR, a federated recommendation framework for learning hierarchical utility-guided client relations. FedHUR takes item-item filters as the object for relation construction and aggregation. Specifically, it first aggregates and clusters each client's local information to obtain global hierarchical information. Each client computes hierarchical utility signals based on its local information and the global hierarchical information, indicating which collaborative information is useful for improving its prediction. The server uses these utility signals to retrieve clients that are useful to that client for further personalized aggregation. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets show that FedHUR consistently outperforms existing federated recommendation baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of hierarchical utility-guided client relation learning. Code is available at https://github.com/Mingzhe-Han/FedHUR.

</details>


### [108] [Your Retriever Already Knows: Distribution-Shape QPP for RAG Retrieval Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11646)
*Matyáš Veselý,Michal Průšek,Jiří Franc*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文比较三种查询性能预测（QPP）范式以判断RAG检索充分性，发现非词法特征方法GeneralQPP在ViDoRe上AUROC 0.856、单查询仅2ms，优于经典QPP池与本地LLM评判，混合方案在核监管数据集SÚJB上取得最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG流水线缺乏可靠的推理期检索成功信号，面对歧义或越界查询时生成易产生幻觉；同时捷克核监管部署场景因数据敏感性禁止使用第三方LLM API，需在本地构建检索充分性判定方案。

Method: 在RAG检索充分性任务上对比三类QPP范式：基于分数特征、基于内容的LLM评判、以及二者的混合。提出24维非词法特征GeneralQPP（15个分布形状特征、5个查询表面特征、4个全局特征），与经典QPP文献特征池（Classic Full）及本地Qwen3.5 LLM评判进行对比，并在SÚJB核监管数据集（含500条合成对抗样本）上验证，另通过留一域（LODO）分析考察跨域泛化。

Result: 在ViDoRe八个视觉域共14,514条查询上，GeneralQPP加权平均AUROC达0.856（2ms/查询），优于Classic Full（0.835），并显著高于本地Qwen3.5 LLM评判（0.649，差距+0.207，且快约3000倍、更便宜）。混合方案在ViDoRe上与S1持平（0.863），但在SÚJB上显著占优：Hit@5下AUROC 0.911，对抗检测0.954（1,510条查询）。两数据集排序一致（Spearman ρ=0.90）。LODO下S1降至0.706，13特征逐步子集S1-Lean回升至0.719（较文献池+0.032）。

Conclusion: 非词法分数型QPP特征（GeneralQPP/S1）可高效、低延迟地预测RAG检索充分性，显著优于经典QPP池与本地LLM评判；混合方案在对分布外与对抗场景敏感的监管数据集上带来统计显著增益。留一域结果表明跨域泛化仍是挑战，需通过特征精简（S1-Lean）缓解。

Abstract: Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines often provide no reliable inference-time signal of whether retrieval succeeded; on ambiguous or out-of-scope queries, generation may then hallucinate. Motivated by a Czech nuclear-regulator deployment where data sensitivity precludes third-party LLM APIs, we compare three Query Performance Prediction (QPP) paradigms for retrieval sufficiency in RAG: score-based features, a content-based LLM judge, and a hybrid. On the eight ViDoRe vision domains (14,514 queries), our 24 non-lexical features (GeneralQPP; 15 distribution-shape, 5 query-surface, 4 global) reach a weighted-average AUROC of 0.856 at 2 ms per query, ahead of a classic-QPP literature pool (Classic Full, 0.835) and well above a local Qwen3.5 LLM judge (0.649, +0.207 gap; $\sim$3000$\times$ faster and cheaper per query). Adding the LLM judgment as one feature (hybrid) matches S1 on ViDoRe (0.863) but gains a statistically significant edge on SÚJB (AUROC 0.911 at Hit@5, adversarial-detection 0.954; 1,510 queries, 500 synthetic adversarial), at LLM latency. Rankings agree across datasets (Spearman $ρ= 0.90$). Under Leave-One-Domain-Out, S1 drops to 0.706; a 13-feature LODO-stepwise subset (S1-Lean) recovers to 0.719 (+0.032 over the literature pool).

</details>


### [109] [Generative Late-Interaction Embeddings For Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11808)
*Mohamed Eltahir,Talal Aloushan,Rose Khairoalsendi,Jana Shata,Mohammed Alhassan,Leen Alrehaili,Tanveer Hussain,Naeemullah Khan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GLIE 利用文档嵌入位于单位球面且内在维度为5–6的几何特性，用每页k个向量作为索引与生成基，查询时仅用k向量检索并对候选页重建全部N向量精确重排，在4向量/页下保留约80%的nDCG@5，优于现有事后压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晚期交互检索是视觉文档搜索的SOTA，但存储开销极大（每页约N~1000个向量）。现有压缩方法仅保留子集或局部平均，在激进存储预算下性能急剧下降，且替代方案需要重训编码器。

Method: 分析三种编码器发现两个一致性质：向量精确落在单位球面上，且集中在本征维度5–6的流形附近。据此提出两点：1) 标准k-means质心落在球内导致MaxSim系统性低估，将其归一化到球面是免费修正（最高+0.093 nDCG@5）；2) 因页面流形自由度低，可由少量向量生成全部向量。由此提出GLIE：每页学习k<<N个向量（由归一化质心初始化），既作轻量索引又作重建基；查询时仅在k个向量上检索，解码器只将top候选页扩展回全部N向量做精确重排。

Result: 在ViDoRe v1上，4向量/页时GLIE保留约80%未压缩系统的nDCG@5，而最佳现有事后方法为70%。仅用415K参数网络、单千页训练、不到3 GPU分钟。在相同训练预算下，微调编码器甚至达不到GLIE的免训练阶段，且全系统在各预算下均胜出。结论在第二个编码器和ViDoRe v2上同样成立。

Conclusion: 通过按需重建证据而非采样证据，GLIE为存储高效检索开辟了新方向，其中解码器是主要设计面。

Abstract: Late-interaction retrieval is the state-of-the-art for visual document search, but it pays for its accuracy in storage. Existing compression methods retain a subset or local average of the N~1,000 vectors per page. Under aggressive storage budgets, however, these methods degrade sharply, and alternatives require retraining the encoder. Investigating this degradation across three encoders, we found two consistent properties: the vectors lie exactly on the unit sphere and concentrate near a manifold of intrinsic dimension five to six. This geometry yields two insights. First, standard k-means centroids fall inside the sphere, causing systematic underestimation of MaxSim scores. Normalizing them to the surface is a free correction worth up to +0.093 nDCG@5 over raw centroids. Second, because the page manifold has few degrees of freedom, the full set of vectors can be regenerated from only a few. To this end, we introduce Generative Late-Interaction Embeddings (GLIE): k << N vectors per page learned from the normalized centroids to serve as both a lightweight index and a basis for regenerating the page's full embedding set. At query time, search runs exclusively on these k vectors, and a decoder expands only the top candidates back to all N vectors for exact rescoring. At four vectors per page on ViDoRe v1, GLIE retains nearly 80% of the uncompressed system's nDCG@5, against 70% for the best prior post-hoc method. These results use a 415K-parameter network fitted in under three GPU-minutes on just a thousand training pages. At a matched training budget, fine-tuning the encoder does not reach even the training-free stage of GLIE, and the full system beats it at every budget. These patterns hold across a second encoder and ViDoRe v2. By reconstructing evidence on demand rather than sampling it, GLIE opens a new axis for storage-efficient retrieval, with the decoder as its main design surface.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [M3-Former: Multimodal Transformer with Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Term Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10559)
*Wenzhe Jin,Haina Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 M3-Former，一种由大语言模型增强的多模态船舶轨迹预测框架，融合静态属性与航行意图语义先验，并采用双粒度 MoE 与转向加权交叉熵损失，在丹麦 AIS 数据集 1–4 小时预测中优于现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对船舶轨迹预测中的行为多模态性、语义信息利用不足以及长期误差累积三大挑战，现有方法难以同时建模全局航路规划与局部操纵变化，且稀疏转向样本的长尾分布影响关键场景精度。

Method: 构建统一多模态表示空间，用预训练 LLM 编码静态语义信息并通过自注意力与动态轨迹特征对齐；引入双粒度 MoE 架构，序列级专家建模全局航行趋势，token 级专家细化细粒度操纵行为；设计 Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy 损失以缓解稀疏转向样本的长尾分布。

Result: 在真实丹麦 AIS 数据集上，1–4 小时预测范围内持续优于 SOTA 基线；4 小时预测任务中，ADE 和 FDE 相比最强基线分别降低 4.4% 和 5.1%；定性与消融实验验证语义融合可有效减少长期轨迹漂移，双粒度 MoE 提升复杂航道与航路分支场景的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了语义引导的分层预测范式，联合建模高层航行意图与局部运动动态，实现鲁棒的长期船舶轨迹预测。

Abstract: To address the challenges of behavioral multimodality, limited semantic utilization, and long-term error accumulation in vessel trajectory prediction, this paper proposes M3-Former, a multimodal trajectory prediction framework enhanced by large language models (LLMs). The proposed framework incorporates vessel static attributes and navigational intent as semantic priors for long-term trajectory modeling. Specifically, a unified multimodal representation space is constructed, in which static semantic information is encoded by a pre-trained LLM and aligned with dynamic trajectory features through self-attention. To jointly capture global route planning and local motion variations, a dual-granularity Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is introduced, where sequence-level experts model global navigation trends and token-level experts refine fine-grained maneuvering behaviors. In addition, a Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy loss is designed to alleviate the long-tail distribution of sparse turning samples and improve prediction accuracy in critical maneuvering scenarios. Experiments on a real-world Danish AIS dataset demonstrate that M\textsuperscript{3}-Former consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across prediction horizons from 1 to 4 hours. In the 4-hour prediction task, the proposed method reduces Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) by 4.4\% and 5.1\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Qualitative and ablation analyses further verify that semantic fusion effectively reduces long-term trajectory drift, while the dual-granularity MoE improves robustness in complex waterways and route-branching scenarios. The proposed framework establishes a semantic-guided hierarchical prediction paradigm, in which high-level navigational intent and local motion dynamics are jointly modeled for robust long-term vessel trajectory forecasting.

</details>


### [111] [Halo: Improving forecast accuracy through heteroscedastic estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10589)
*Adam Cataldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Halo：在深度预测器中增加一个尺度（scale）输出并用负对数似然训练，在时间序列点预测上不仅量化不确定性，还显著改善点估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异方差预测（同时估计位置参数与尺度参数）通常被认为用于不确定性量化，而在非时间序列任务上已有负面结果，暗示其对点估计无益。作者试图检验：在时间序列中，估计尺度是否能反过来提升点估计，并解释与已有负面结论的差异。

Method: Halo 复用现有深度预测器架构，为其增加第二个输出以表示所隐含分布的尺度，并在匹配的负对数似然损失下联合训练。作者将三种 SOTA 模型（Transformer、结合变分自编码器的图网络、单层卷积网络）在 Gaussian 与 Laplacian 损失下进行适配，并在标准预测基准的五个电力价格市场上进行 3×5×2 组对照实验。

Result: 在 30 组“模型-市场-指标”比较中，Halo 有 28 组改善了 MSE 与 MAE；平均 MSE 降低 2.6%~16.5%，平均 MAE 降低 1.7%~11.0%。两项发现：(1) 尺度估计来自第二个投影头还是完整并行网络影响甚微，关键在于网络是否估计尺度；(2) 提升在已为点估计基线调好的超参数下依然成立，重调参非必需。

Conclusion: 在时间序列预测中，估计尺度参数的异方差建模可同时提升点估计与不确定性刻画，且与已有非时间序列负面结果并不矛盾；该现象跨模型、跨损失、跨市场稳健，且无需额外调参，具有较低的应用成本。

Abstract: Heteroscedastic forecasting, where a network estimates a scale parameter alongside a location parameter, is normally motivated by uncertainty quantification. This paper shows it also improves the point estimate, in contrast to reported negative results for heteroscedastic estimation outside time series. Halo is a modification that reuses an existing deep forecaster's architecture, giving it a second output for the scale of its implied distribution and training it under the matching negative log likelihood. Adapting three state-of-the-art models --- a transformer, a graph network paired with a variational autoencoder, and a single-layer convolutional network --- under both Gaussian and Laplacian losses demonstrates the phenomenon. On the five electricity price markets of a standard forecasting benchmark, Halo improves MSE and MAE in 28 of 30 model-market-metric comparisons, cutting average MSE by 2.6% to 16.5% and average MAE by 1.7% to 11.0%. Two findings emerge: (1) whether the scale estimate comes from a second projection head or from a full parallel network matters far less than whether the network estimates scale, and (2) the improvement holds under the hyperparameters already tuned for the point-estimate baseline, so retuning is optional.

</details>


### [112] [Zero-shot rib design: merging training-free generative prior with topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10643)
*Yongmin Kwon,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型作为免训练的设计知识源，通过分数蒸馏采样把文本提示注入密度法拓扑优化的物理循环，使自然语言成为可机读的工程意图表示，在四个几何域、两种物理场中共245次主SDS实验中，38/49个提示-域组合取得显著柔度下降（力学最高−31.5%，热弹性−23.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界承受载荷的结构（叶脉、骨小梁、蛛网）具有极高的单位质量刚度，但经典拓扑优化器很少生成此类几何，且几乎不能通过自然语言表达设计意图。作者希望让工程师用文本提示直接表达结构设计意图，并把预训练生成模型作为可复用、免训练的先验引入工程设计。

Method: 把冻结的文本到图像扩散模型当作免训练的设计知识源，通过分数蒸馏采样（SDS）将生成先验蒸馏进密度法拓扑优化的物理循环：每次迭代中，将提示诱导的生成梯度与有限元灵敏度相结合，由物理决定哪些提示诱导特征得以保留。采用Heaviside投影配合β-续延（β-continuation）处理扩散-物理耦合中显著的中间密度倾向，并用自动化骨架提取流程把优化密度场转换为可进入CAD的候选几何。通过更换文本提示实现跨域、跨载荷、跨物理目标的先验重定向，各新问题的物理设置单独指定。

Result: 在四个几何域、两种物理场下共245次主SDS运行中，49个提示-域组合里有38个实现统计显著的柔度下降，力学最高−31.5%、热弹性最高−23.0%，优于基于梯度的基线。跨域形态分析发现一个反复出现的改善结构特征：多数域中生成先验抑制了肋骨架中的死端分支，端点-柔度相关性为r=+0.56至+0.99。Heaviside投影配合β-续延将该扩散-物理耦合中明显的中间密度倾向从42.6%降至<3%。

Conclusion: 文本提示可作为显式、机器可解释的工程意图表示，冻结的生成模型能被直接作为免训练、可复用的设计先验注入拓扑优化的物理循环；该框架通过更换提示即可跨域、跨载荷与跨物理目标重定向，把预训练生成模型转变为可复用的工程设计先验，并输出可进入CAD的候选几何。

Abstract: Natural load-bearing patterns such as leaf venation, trabecular bone, and spider webs achieve high stiffness per unit mass, yet classical topology optimizers rarely reach such geometries, and few let engineers express structural design intent through natural language. This work treats a frozen text-to-image diffusion model as a training-free source of design knowledge and distills it into the physics loop of density-based topology optimization via score distillation sampling, so that a text prompt becomes an explicit, machine-interpretable representation of engineer intent. The prompt-induced generative gradient and the finite element sensitivity are combined at every iteration, letting physics decide which prompt-induced features survive. In 245 primary SDS runs spanning four geometric domains and two physics regimes, 38 of 49 prompt--domain combinations achieved statistically significant compliance reductions (up to $-31.5\%$ mechanical and $-23.0\%$ thermoelastic), outperforming gradient-based baselines. Cross-domain morphological analysis identifies a recurring structural signature of improvement: in most domains the generative prior suppresses dead-end branches in the rib skeleton, with endpoint--compliance correlation $r = +0.56$ to $+0.99$. A Heaviside projection with $β$-continuation resolves a pronounced intermediate-density tendency in this diffusion--physics coupling ($42.6\%$ to $<3\%$), and an automated skeleton-based pipeline converts optimized density fields into \rev{candidate geometry ready for computer-aided design. By retargeting the generative prior across domains, loading conditions, and physics objectives through a change of text prompt, with each new problem's physics setup specified separately, the framework uses a pretrained generative model as a reusable, training-free prior for engineering design.

</details>


### [113] [Byzantine-Robust Federated Fire Detection with a Rotating Coordinator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10647)
*Georgia Argyrou,Aymen Bahrouny,Hedi Fendriy,Alexander Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将联邦学习应用于室内火灾检测，针对上行带宽受限、拜占庭客户端和固定聚合服务器单点信任三大障碍，提出数据集构建、模型压缩与半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒聚合三项贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内火灾检测依赖边缘摄像头，其敏感视频难以集中上传至中心服务器，因此适合联邦学习。但现有联邦方案忽视三个实际问题：上行带宽有限、存在恶意或故障的拜占庭客户端、以及无条件信任单一且永久固定的聚合服务器。

Method: （i）从八个公开来源整理构建室内火灾检测数据集；（ii）设计可边缘部署的检测器，将模型更新压缩至最多10倍而上行传输开销大幅降低；（iii）提出半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法，结合历史感知聚合与轮换协调者机制。

Result: 模型更新压缩最多10倍仅带来平衡准确率的小幅损失；轮换协调者方法能剔除逐轮过滤器漏检的隐蔽攻击，并消除固定服务器单点故障；在留出测试集上，其准确率与检测速度与固定服务器版本相当；六节点物理分布式云部署验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 所提方案在保障室内火灾检测性能的同时，同时缓解带宽、拜占庭攻击与服务器单点信任问题，并通过实际分布式部署证明了工程可行性。

Abstract: We study the application of federated learning (FL) to indoor fire detection. Such fire-detection systems use edge cameras that record sensitive footage which cannot easily be collected at a central server. Existing federated solutions leave three practical obstacles unaddressed: limited uplink bandwidth, Byzantine (malicious or faulty) clients, and unconditional trust in a single, permanently fixed aggregation server. Our main contributions address all three. In particular, we provide (i) a curated indoor fire-detection dataset assembled from eight public sources; (ii) an edge-deployable detector whose model updates are compressed up to 10 time with only a small loss in balanced accuracy; and (iii) a semi-decentralized Byzantine-robust FL method that combines history-aware aggregation with a rotating coordinator, evicting stealthy attacks that per-round filters miss while removing the fixed-server single point of failure. On the held-out test set the rotating-coordinator method matches its fixed-server counterpart in accuracy and detection speed, and a physically distributed six-node cloud deployment confirms feasibility.

</details>


### [114] [Artificial Intelligence Algorithms for the Detection of Pathologies Related to Lung Cancer through Image Analysis using Convolutional Neural Networks and Data Augmentation: a systematic mapping of the literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10652)
*Pablo Ramirez Amador*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述2015年以来AI与深度学习在肺癌影像检测中的应用，重点介绍CNN结合迁移学习与数据增强的优势，并指出数据标准化、可解释性、隐私与伦理等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球主要致死原因之一，早期诊断对改善患者预后至关重要；但医学影像判读复杂且依赖专业受训人员，因此需要借助AI与深度学习实现影像分析的自动化与优化。

Method: 在PubMed、IEEEXPLORE、Scopus和Web of Science等科学数据库中进行 exhaustive 检索，筛选出96篇2015年至今涉及AI与深度学习在生物医学工程中应用的文章，进行综述分析。

Result: 结果显示，AI与深度学习可为肺癌早期诊断提供有效替代方案，具有较高的敏感性和特异性；其中卷积神经网络（CNN）结合迁移学习和数据增强是提升影像判读准确性与效率的前景技术。

Conclusion: AI与深度学习可对肺癌患者诊疗产生积极影响，但尚需进一步研究与监管，以应对标准化数据缺乏、模型可解释性、患者隐私及伦理社会影响等问题，确保其质量与可靠性。

Abstract: Lung cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, and its early diagnosis is crucial to improving patients prognosis and quality of life. However, the process of interpreting medical images for the detection of lung cancer is complex and requires trained experts. In this context, artificial intelligence (AI) and deep learning (DL) emerge as potential tools to automate and optimize image analysis. The objective of this work is to review the most recent and relevant applications of AI and DL in the field of radiology for the detection of lung cancer. To this end, an exhaustive search was carried out in scientific databases such as PubMed,IEEEXPLORE, Scopus and Web of Science, and 96 articles published from 2015 to the present addressing the use of AI and DL in biomedical engineering were selected. Emphasis is placed on the use of convolutional neural networks (CNN) with transfer learning and Data Augmentation as promising techniques to improve the accuracy and efficiency of the image interpretation process. The results show that the use of AI and DL can offer an effective alternative for the early diagnosis of lung cancer, with high sensitivity and specificity. However, current limitations and challenges that must be addressed to guarantee its responsible and safe application in clinical practice are also identified, such as the lack of standardized data, the ex plainability of the models, patient privacy, and the ethical and social implications. It is concluded that the use of AI and DL can have a positive impact on the care of patients with lung cancer, but further research and regulation are required to ensure its quality and reliability.

</details>


### [115] [GEOSTEER: Geodesic Optimization for Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10658)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo,Hao Vo,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoSteer，一种基于优化、保持激活范数的激活引导方法：将引导建模为黎曼优化问题，通过表示流形上的一系列测地线小步长更新激活，并学习非线性目标函数自适应引导每一步，在TruthfulQA、RealToxicityPrompts和UltraFeedback上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导能在推理阶段通过修改隐藏激活来控制大语言模型，其中保范引导可在不改变激活范数的前提下改变模型行为，降低表示坍缩与性能退化的风险。但现有保范方法受限于预定义的引导轨迹和单步更新，难以刻画激活分布的复杂结构。

Method: GeoSteer将引导建模为黎曼优化问题，在表示流形上以一系列小幅测地线步长更新激活；同时学习一个非线性激活空间目标函数以区分期望与不期望的激活，并据此自适应地引导每一步引导方向，避免固定引导方向，形成多步引导框架。

Result: 在TruthfulQA、RealToxicityPrompts和UltraFeedback三个基准上，GeoSteer持续优于当前最先进的激活引导基线，并实现更平滑、稳定且一致的引导行为，同时保持激活范数不变。

Conclusion: 用几何感知的自适应多步优化替代预定义的单步编辑，可使保范激活引导更有效。这表明在保持激活范数的约束下，引入流形几何与自适应优化能显著提升引导质量。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight way to control large language models (LLMs) by modifying their hidden activations at inference time. Among these approaches, norm-preserving steering aims to change model behavior without altering the activation norm, reducing the risk of representation collapse and degradation. However, existing norm-preserving methods are limited by predefined steering trajectories and by their reliance on one-step updates, which may fail to capture the complex structure of activation distributions. We propose GeoSteer, an optimization-based method for norm-preserving activation steering. GeoSteer formulates steering as a Riemannian optimization problem and updates activations through a sequence of small geodesic steps on the representation manifold. To avoid fixed steering directions, GeoSteer learns a nonlinear activation-space objective that distinguishes desired from undesired activations, and uses this function to adaptively guide each steering step. This multistep formulation yields smoother, more stable, and more consistent steering behavior while preserving the activation norm. Across TruthfulQA, RealToxicityPrompts, and UltraFeedback benchmarks, GeoSteer consistently improves over state-of-the-art activation steering baselines. These results suggest that norm-preserving steering can be made more effective by replacing predefined one-step edits with adaptive, geometry-aware optimization.

</details>


### [116] [Conformal Calibration Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10737)
*Achref Doula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究"保形校准迁移"（conformal calibration transfer）问题：当标注校准数据仅存在于源空间、而预测集需在通过无标注配对观测关联的目标空间中给出时，如何在目标域实现有限样本覆盖保证，提出 TCC 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保形预测（conformal prediction）的覆盖保证依赖校准数据与部署数据之间的可交换性（exchangeability）。但在许多实际场景（如配对多模态、传感器变化）中，标注校准样本只在源空间可得，目标空间仅有未标注的配对观测，可交换性假设失效，因此需要研究如何将源域校准迁移到目标域并保持覆盖有效性。

Method: 提出 Transported Conformal Calibration（TCC）：先用配对数据把源域标注校准迁移到目标空间，再仅利用无标注目标输入来修正迁移后的残余失配。（1）TCC-KS：采用无标签的不确定性代理量检测失配，并以保守方式调整校准；（2）weighted-TCC：在权重稳定时将迁移校准重加权指向目标域以提高效率。理论上给出随可观测失配度量自适应的目标域有限样本覆盖保证。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100-C、Tiny-ImageNet-C 与 SEN12MS 上验证：无需标注的目标域校准数据即可实现可靠的目标域覆盖迁移，且无标签诊断指标能够预测何时需要修正。

Conclusion: TCC 为校准迁移场景提供了可行方案，在无可交换性、无标注目标校准的条件下依然能够维持目标域的覆盖有效性，并通过无标签诊断指导修正策略的选择。

Abstract: Conformal prediction converts point predictions into set-valued predictions with coverage guarantees under exchangeability between calibration and deployment data. We study conformal calibration transfer, where this requirement fails because labeled calibration is available only in a source space, while prediction sets are needed in a target space linked to the source through unlabeled paired observations (e.g., paired modalities or sensor changes). We propose Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC): we transport labeled source calibration into the target space using the paired data, and then correct residual post-transport mismatch using only unlabeled target inputs. We instantiate this correction with two complementary methods: TCC-KS, which uses a label-free uncertainty surrogate to detect mismatch and adjust calibration conservatively, and weighted-TCC, which reweights transported calibration toward the target domain for improved efficiency when weights are stable. We provide finite-sample target-domain coverage guarantees that adapt to an observable measure of mismatch. Across CIFAR-100-C, Tiny-ImageNet-C, and SEN12MS, we show reliable target-domain coverage transfer without labeled target calibration data, with label-free diagnostics that predict when correction is needed.

</details>


### [117] [The Truth Was Never Gone: Perfect Aliasing in Compliant-Context Truth Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10739)
*Dylan Jayabahu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“完美混叠”（perfect aliasing）概念：当真实性与任务规定动作在合规语境中重合时，探针无法仅凭拟合标签区分二者；研究通过随机码本与混合语境拟合将其分离，并在Gemma-2-9B策略上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在合规语境中，“真实报告”与“任务规定动作”重合，导致仅在合规样本上拟合的探针无法从标签本身实现语义识别。作者欲揭示这一识别失效并提出区分二者的方法。

Method: 构建受控二元报告博弈；先在合规语境上分别拟合真实性探针与规定动作探针，比较其优化目标与AUROC关系；再用随机码本将规定输出符号与语义动作分离，并通过混合合规与对手语境拟合来分离真实性与规定动作；最后在奖励训练的Gemma-2-9B策略上进行验证。

Result: 在对手语境下两类探针标签互补，其AUROC之和恒为1，该恒等式在751个cell-layer对上达到浮点精度；常规探针在三个训练种子下对全部对手试验仅得0.006±0.005 AUROC，而混合拟合探针在同一留出激活上达到1.000；两个分布内均完美的合规拟合探针在相同对手激活上分别得0.080和0.986。

Conclusion: 该发现说明探针“测量的是什么”，但未证明功能性信念被保留、所恢复方向的因果作用或可部署的欺骗检测器；混合拟合因使用更多样本且需标注对手语境，故该比较仅说明线性可恢复性，而非隔离去相关的收益。

Abstract: A truth probe fitted where truthful reporting and a task's prescribed action coincide cannot distinguish those targets from its fitting labels alone. We call this failure of semantic identification perfect aliasing. In a controlled binary reporting game, truth and prescribed-action probes fitted on compliant contexts solve the same optimization. On rival contexts their labels are complements, forcing their AUROCs to sum to one; this identity holds across 751 cell-layer pairs to floating-point precision. We separate prescribed output symbols from semantic action using randomized codebooks, then separate truth from prescribed action by fitting on mixed compliant and rival contexts. For a reward-trained Gemma-2-9B policy that answers falsely on all evaluated rival trials, the conventional probe scores $0.006 \pm 0.005$ AUROC across three training seeds, while mixed-fit probes score $1.000$ on the same held-out activations. Mixed fitting uses more training examples and access to labelled rival contexts, so this comparison establishes linear recoverability rather than isolating the benefit of decorrelation. We also show that two compliant-fit probes, both perfect in-distribution, score $0.080$ and $0.986$ on the same rival activations. The findings concern what a probe measures: they do not establish preserved functional belief, causal use of the recovered direction, or a deployable deception detector. Code and aggregate results accompany the paper.

</details>


### [118] [Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss: Penalizing Center-Class Hedging in Ordinal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10752)
*Manisha Kandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应间隔序数损失（AMOL），通过乘性权重抑制标准交叉熵在序数分类中的"中心类对冲"失效模式，并引入中心对冲率（CHR）作为诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失在序数分类任务中会使网络将预测向中心类偏移，因为预测中间类可最小化对称损失的期望，成为阻力最小的路径，这一失效模式被称为"中心类对冲"。已有的序数损失虽处理了大误差惩罚和秩一致性等相关问题，但没有一种方法能根据真实标签相对于序数中心的位置直接抑制中心类对冲。

Method: 提出自适应间隔序数损失（AMOL），对逐类损失项施加乘性权重 m(k,y) = 1 + α·(1 - |k-c|/c)·(|y-c|/c)，其中 c 为中心类，k 为候选类，y 为真实标签。该权重编码联合条件：仅当候选类靠近中心且真实标签远离中心时权重较大，否则退化为标准行为。同时引入中心对冲率（CHR）作为直接量化该失效模式的诊断指标。

Result: 在四个序数分类基准和五个随机种子上，AMOL 在全部四个数据集上取得最优或并列最优的二次加权 Kappa（QWK），优于交叉熵、OLL 和 SORD 基线。非对称变体（AMOL-asym）在 Abalone 数据集上完全消除了中心类对冲（五个种子 CHR 均为 0.000±0.000，每次运行约 266 个极端类测试样本），而标准交叉熵为 0.074±0.005。

Conclusion: AMOL 通过联合条件加权有效抑制了中心类对冲，在多个基准上达到最优或并列最优性能，其非对称变体可彻底消除该失效模式，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy loss causes neural networks trained on ordinal classification tasks to hedge predictions toward center classes, a failure mode we term \emph{center-class hedging}. This occurs because predicting the middle class minimizes expected symmetric loss, making it the path of least resistance regardless of the true label. Existing ordinal losses address related problems such as large-error penalization and rank consistency, but none directly suppresses center-class hedging as a function of where the true label lies relative to the ordinal center. We propose the Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss (AMOL), a multiplicative weight applied to per-class loss terms of the form $m(k,y) = 1 + α\cdot (1 - |k-c|/c) \cdot (|y-c|/c)$, where $c$ is the center class, $k$ is the candidate class, and $y$ is the true label. The weight encodes a joint condition: it is large only when the candidate class is near center and the true label is far from center, collapsing to standard behavior otherwise. We further introduce the Center-Hedging Rate (CHR) as a diagnostic metric that directly quantifies this failure mode. Across four ordinal classification benchmarks and five random seeds, AMOL achieves the best or tied-best Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) on all four datasets compared to cross-entropy, OLL, and SORD baselines. An asymmetric variant (AMOL-asym) eliminates center-class hedging entirely on the Abalone dataset ($\text{CHR} = 0.000 \pm 0.000$ across all five seeds, $n \approx 266$ extreme-class test samples per run), compared to $0.074 \pm 0.005$ for standard cross-entropy.

</details>


### [119] [REVA: Reusable Evidence View Aggregation for Context-Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11209)
*Tuan Nguyen,Qiran Hu,Banruo Liu,Khoa D. Doan,Kok-Seng Wong,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REVA框架，从数据挖掘视角将历史查询-文档-模型交互聚合为可复用证据视图，实现高效检索后压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG因长上下文带来延迟、KV缓存与token成本上升，检索后压缩虽可降本，但现有压缩器逐查询独立运行、依赖辅助模型或重写，且在线开销抵消了短提示的收益。

Method: REVA将目标生成器的历史注意力轨迹挖掘为以文档为键、与预算无关的分数存储；把token级注意力映射为可读词单元，跨重复文档访问聚合重要性，并渲染保留文档顺序与标准RAG接口的预算特定纯文本视图。

Result: 在四个代表性基准与现代LLM上，生成质量较现有先进方法提升1.0–5.8分，压缩开销降低5.3–15.6倍，额外延迟低于40毫秒；同时揭示现代压缩器相对简单截断收益不稳定且增加推理延迟。

Conclusion: 通过复用历史交互证据而非逐查询在线压缩，REVA在质量与效率间取得更优权衡，为RAG压缩提供了数据挖掘式的新思路。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves knowledge-intensive large language model (LLM) applications by conditioning generation on retrieved documents, but longer contexts increase latency, key-value (KV) cache memory, and token cost. Post-retrieval compression can reduce this cost, yet existing compressors often operate independently for each query, rely on auxiliary models or rewriting, and introduce online overhead that can offset the benefit of shorter prompts. We revisit RAG compression from a data-mining perspective by aggregating historical query--document--model interactions into reusable evidence views. We first show that modern compressors have unstable gains over simple truncation and can add substantial inference-time latency. We then propose Reusable Evidence View Aggregation (REVA), a framework that mines the target generator's historical attention traces into a document-keyed, budget-agnostic score store. REVA maps token-level attention to readable word units, aggregates importance across repeated document accesses, and renders budget-specific plain-text views that preserve document order and the standard RAG interface. Across four representative benchmarks and modern LLMs, REVA improves generation quality by 1.0--5.8 points over existing advances, while reducing compression overhead by a factor of 5.3 to 15.6, adding less than 40 ms of latency.

</details>


### [120] [A Bellman Optimality Equation for Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10776)
*Jeremy Lucas,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是针对"持续强化学习中可塑性优化"的初步研究，证明在MDP中优化可塑性存在与赋能类似的最优贝尔曼方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续强化学习中，稳定性-可塑性权衡是核心挑战。Abel等(2025)将该权衡形式化为赋能-可塑性权衡（可塑性为观测到动作的广义有向信息，赋能为动作到观测的广义有向信息）。然而关于赋能优化已有大量文献，在新定义下优化可塑性的研究却尚属空白。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）框架下研究可塑性的优化问题，构造并分析可塑性的贝尔曼最优性方程。

Result: 证明了与赋能优化工作类似，针对可塑性优化也存在相应的贝尔曼最优性方程。

Conclusion: 本文为在赋能-可塑性权衡新定义下优化可塑性提供了初步的理论基础，即存在可塑性的贝尔曼最优性方程。

Abstract: In continual reinforcement learning, carefully managing the stability-plasticity tradeoff remains a core challenge. Recent work by Abel et al. (2025) formalized this dilemma by defining plasticity as the generalized directed information from an agent's observations to its actions, and empowerment as the generalized directed information from its actions to its observations. This formulation successfully reframes the traditional stability-plasticity tradeoff as an empowerment-plasticity tradeoff. However, while extensive literature exists on optimizing for empowerment, there is currently no research addressing the optimization of plasticity under this new definition. This paper presents preliminary work toward optimizing plasticity within Markov decision processes. We show that there exists a Bellman optimality equation for optimizing plasticity similar to previous work for empowerment.

</details>


### [121] [Counterfactual Marginalisation: Framework for Evaluating Robustness to Nuisance Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10778)
*Yasin Ibrahim,Hermione Warr,Robin J. Evans,Konstantinos Kamnitsas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出反事实边缘化（CF marginalisation）作为测试时评估方法，利用CF图像生成器干预年龄、性别等干扰变量并按目标干预分布平均预测，从而评估分类模型对人口学/采集相关捷径的鲁棒性，并定义CF风险、校准、稳定性与最坏情况敏感度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型可能在测试集上表现优异，却依赖人口学或采集相关的捷径特征，而非真实的判别性信息。现有评估方式难以量化此类捷径对模型鲁棒性的影响。

Method: 给定一个反事实图像生成器，对每个测试图像干预其干扰父变量（如年龄、性别），生成对应的反事实版本；在目标干预分布上对预测进行平均，得到干预感知预测，在边缘化人口学效应的同时保留患者特异性潜在信息；基于该预测构建反事实风险、校准、稳定性和最坏情况敏感度等指标。

Result: 该框架可用于对模型的鲁棒性进行定量评估，能够揭示模型对人口学与采集相关捷径变量的依赖程度。

Conclusion: 反事实边缘化为分类模型的捷径鲁棒性提供了一种测试时、可量化的评估手段，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Machine learning models can achieve strong test performance while relying on demographic or acquisition-related shortcuts. We propose counterfactual (CF) marginalisation as a test-time evaluation procedure for assessing robustness of classification models to such variables. Given a CF image generator, we intervene on nuisance parent variables such as age or sex, generate CF versions of each test image, and average predictions over a target intervention distribution. This produces intervention-aware predictions that marginalise demographic effects while preserving patient-specific latent information. We use these predictions to define metrics for CF risk, calibration, stability and worst-case sensitivity. We demonstrate this framework's utility for quantitative robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [122] [From Connectivity to Rewards: Dense Reward Learning with Directed State Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10781)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Zihan Wang,Xiao-Wen Chang,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出G2QDR框架，通过状态连通性模型预测非对称环境中的状态连通强度，并将其转化为标量辅助稠密奖励，为GCHRL提供多层级连续引导，可理论集成到任意现有GCHRL架构中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的GCHRL方法常忽视图的内在连通性信息，仅将图作为随机采样工具，而非编码连通性与状态可达性的环境模型；在准度量环境中，状态转移的固有非对称性对稳定策略学习和鲁棒路径规划构成根本挑战。

Method: 引入状态连通性模型，预测非对称环境下的成对状态连通强度；将连通强度转化为标量辅助稠密奖励，在多个层级提供连续引导；通过在有向状态图上训练的神经网络高效实现该模型，该图在探索过程中生成。

Result: 在多种稀疏奖励环境中的实证结果表明，G2QDR总体上能以可接受的计算开销提升基线GCHRL方法的性能。

Conclusion: G2QDR可理论集成至任意现有GCHRL架构，利用连通性作为稠密奖励信号缓解准度量环境中的非对称性难题，并有效增强基线方法的性能。

Abstract: The integration of graphs with Goal-Conditioned Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (GCHRL) has received increasing attention, as graphs naturally encode task hierarchies for effective subgoal sampling. However, existing methods often overlook intrinsic connectivity information, failing to fully leverage the underlying topology for efficient learning. Most graph-based GCHRL methods use the graph as a stochastic sampling tool rather than as an environmental model that encodes connectivity and state-accessibility information. This limitation is particularly acute in quasimetric environments, where the inherent asymmetry of state transitions poses a fundamental challenge to stable policy learning and robust path planning. In this paper, we address these problems by introducing a state connectivity model designed to predict pairwise state connectivity strength in asymmetric environments. We transform these connectivity strengths into scalar auxiliary dense rewards, providing continuous guidance across multiple hierarchical levels. We demonstrate that our proposed framework, Graph-Guided Quasimetric Dense Reward (G2QDR), can theoretically be integrated into any existing GCHRL architecture, and the state connectivity model is efficiently implemented via a neural network trained on a directed state graph generated during exploration. Empirical results across a wide range of sparse reward environments indicate that, in general, G2QDR can enhance the performance of baseline GCHRL approaches with acceptable computational overhead.

</details>


### [123] [DR-LabStack: Design and Implementation of a Clinician-Facing Web System for Diabetic Retinopathy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10796)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文设计并实现了 DR-LabStack——一个 React-Flask Web 系统，用于集成四个异构的糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）预训练模型，实现统一表单交互、后端适配与标准化输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DR 预训练模型在输入字段、序列化格式、预处理要求和输出语义上各不相同，缺乏统一的临床交互接口，导致模型难以被便捷地访问与协同使用，因此需要在用户界面与推理服务之间进行显式协调。

Method: 构建 React-Flask Web 系统，集成 RuleFit、Pruned RuleFit、Elaborative XGBoost 与 Two-level Ensemble 四个外部模型。采用共享表单按序获取模型特征，渲染数值与类别控件并构造位置式输入向量；后端适配器加载异构模型工件并应用集成模型配套的 scaler；统一 JSON 响应支持二分类结果展示及方法与来源信息。

Result: 在 2026 年 9 月 8 日的隔离环境中，使用真实模型工件进行功能评估：四个模型均成功加载并暴露出 14、6、8、25 字段的输入契约；62 次 Flask 测试客户端请求刻画了服务行为；12 次有限向量检查验证了调用路径与阈值一致性；24 个带 mock 传输的浏览器组件场景验证了输入顺序与结果渲染，并刻画了输入校验行为。

Conclusion: 该系统为异构 DR 模型提供了可复用的交互与服务化工作流，其主要贡献在于 Web 系统设计、集成与软件功能实现；临床有效性与临床医生可用性仍需另行评估。

Abstract: Pretrained diabetic retinopathy (DR) prediction models differ in their input fields, serialization formats, preprocessing requirements, and output semantics. Making these models accessible through a common clinical interface therefore requires explicit coordination between the user interface and the inference service. We designed and implemented DR-LabStack, a React-Flask web system integrating four externally developed pretrained models: RuleFit, Pruned RuleFit, Elaborative XGBoost, and Two-level Ensemble. A shared form retrieves ordered model features, renders model-specific numerical and categorical controls, and constructs a positional input vector. Backend adapters load heterogeneous artifacts and apply the ensemble's accompanying scaler, while a common JSON response supports binary classification display alongside method and source information. Functional evaluation on September 8, 2026 used copied application files and real model artifacts in a documented isolated environment. All four models loaded and exposed their 14-, 6-, 8-, and 25-field contracts. Sixty-two Flask test-client requests characterized service behavior; 12 limited-vector checks confirmed invocation-path and threshold consistency. Twenty-four browser-component scenarios with mocked transport verified input ordering and result rendering and characterized input-validation behavior. The resulting system demonstrates a reusable interaction and serving workflow for heterogeneous DR models. The contribution is web-system design, integration, and software functionality; clinical effectiveness and clinician usability require separate evaluation.

</details>


### [124] [RiVaT-Fuse: Reliability-Calibrated Variational Tensor Fusion for Multimodal Prediction under Modality Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10798)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang,Taiping Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RiVaT-Fuse 将多模态融合重新定义为样本级隐状态估计：通过可靠性校准的变分张量目标，在图像证据、元数据证据、结构化跨模态交互与稳定性之间取得平衡，替代传统向量拼接式融合，并在图像级元数据预测基准上取得最优综合预测排名且扰动下更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像-元数据预测需融合异构证据，其可靠性随样本与潜在因素变化。现有表征级融合方法（拼接、门控、条件调制、注意力）只选择聚合架构，未显式定义模态不确定下融合表征应具有何种含义，缺乏可靠性建模的理论依据。

Method: 提出 RiVaT-Fuse 可靠性校准变分张量融合框架：将融合定义为样本级隐状态估计，通过变分目标估计共识隐状态，平衡图像证据、元数据证据、结构化跨模态交互与稳定性；以矩阵值信任几何替代标量模态置信度；将交互分解为加性、乘性与关系型三部分；将隐状态与条件鲁棒性及结构化多任务预测耦合；给出隐式求解的适定性与稳定性解释，并用高效的低秩+对角信任算子实例化。

Result: 在图像级图像-元数据预测基准上，RiVaT-Fuse 在直接表征级基线中取得最强总体预测排名，同时在扰动条件下提升了概率与标签稳定性。

Conclusion: 融合应被视为带可靠性校准的隐状态估计问题而非简单聚合；矩阵值信任几何与结构化交互分解为模态不确定性下的融合提供了可解释且稳定的建模路径。

Abstract: Image-metadata prediction requires fusing heterogeneous evidence whose reliability can vary across samples and latent factors. Existing representation-level fusion methods typically choose an aggregation architecture, such as concatenation, gating, conditional modulation, or attention, without explicitly defining what the fused representation should mean under modality uncertainty. We propose RiVaT-Fuse, a reliability-calibrated variational tensor fusion framework that defines fusion as sample-wise latent-state estimation. Rather than producing a fused vector by direct aggregation, RiVaT-Fuse estimates a consensus latent state through a variational objective that balances image evidence, metadata evidence, structured cross-modal interaction, and stability. The resulting framework replaces scalar modality confidence with matrix-valued trust geometry, decomposes interaction into additive, multiplicative, and relational components, and couples the latent state with conditional robustness and structured multi-task prediction. We provide well-posedness and stability interpretations of the latent solve and instantiate the framework with efficient low-rank-plus-diagonal trust operators. On an image-level image-metadata prediction benchmark, RiVaT-Fuse achieves the strongest overall predictive rank among direct representation-level baselines while improving probability and label stability under perturbation.

</details>


### [125] [Processing and classifying bird songs using wavelet techniques and supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10826)
*Laura Lucia Dominguez Barrios,Fidel Aniano Causil Barrios,Alex Rodrigo dos Santos Sousa,Mariana Rodrigues Motta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向高噪声自然声景中入侵鸟类鸣声的贝叶斯小波去噪与分类集成框架，基于Epanechnikov核先验的小波收缩方法对三种鸟类录音去噪后提取MFCC及谱特征，SVM在10维MFCC配置下取得最高0.9398的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然声景中环境噪声强，导致入侵鸟类鸣声信号退化，影响自动识别与生态监测；同时大规模生物声学数据需要计算高效且具有闭式解的去噪方法。

Method: 采用基于Epanechnikov核先验的贝叶斯小波收缩方法进行信号去噪，该方法具有闭式决策规则与高计算效率；对iNaturalist平台上的Euphonia violacea、Leiothrix lutea和Passer domesticus三种鸟类录音进行处理，提取MFCC以及熵、过零率等谱指数特征，并在不同特征维度下评估随机森林、多项逻辑回归与支持向量机等监督学习模型。

Result: 所提出的小波预处理显著提升了分类性能，SVM模型在10维MFCC配置下取得最高准确率（达0.9398）。

Conclusion: 该研究为自动化生态监测和生物入侵管理提供了一种稳健的统计工具。

Abstract: This study proposes an integrated framework for the processing and classification of invasive bird species vocalizations within natural soundscapes, characterized by high levels of environmental noise. We address the challenge of signal degradation by employing a Bayesian wavelet shrinkage methodology based on the Epanechnikov kernel prior, which offers a closed form decision rule and high computational efficiency for processing large bioacoustic datasets. The methodology was applied to recordings of three species obtained from the iNaturalist platform: \textit{Euphonia violacea}, \textit{Leiothrix lutea}, and \textit{Passer domesticus}. After signal denoising, we extracted a comprehensive set of features, including Mel-Frequency Cepstral Coefficients (MFCCs) and spectral indices such as entropy and zero-crossing rate. Several supervised learning models: Random Forest, Multinomial Logistic Regression and Support Vector Machine (SVM) were evaluated across different feature dimensionalities. Our results demonstrate that the proposed wavelet based preprocessing significantly enhances classification performance, with the SVM model achieving the highest accuracy (up to 0.9398) under a 10-dimensional MFCC configuration. This research provides a robust statistical tool for automated ecological monitoring and the management of biological invasions.

</details>


### [126] [Flow Duality and Source Geometry for Categorical Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10863)
*Etrit Haxholli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了连续流匹配与离散流匹配之间的对偶关系：对具有one-hot目标的连续凸插值路径逐位置取argmax，可得到离散凸插值路径，该结论在源分布满足坐标对称性与边界正则性时成立，并使连续源分布成为类别生成中的显式设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续流匹配与离散流匹配通常被视为两套独立构造，二者之间缺乏统一的理论联系；同时，类别生成中连续源分布的选择往往被忽视，缺少对其如何影响离散插值行为的系统分析。

Method: 通过逐位置argmax将连续凸插值路径（one-hot目标）投影为离散凸插值路径，建立二者的对偶关系，并要求源分布满足坐标对称性与边界正则性；随后分别推导高斯、有界均匀及中心化负指数三类源所诱导的离散插值行为，并辅以小型可视化诊断与简短语言建模试验。

Result: 证明了对偶关系成立，并表明不同源几何会导致定性上不同的转移时序与词表规模依赖性；可视化诊断与语言建模初步试验显示，这些源设计效应也会体现在学习到的传输过程及早期生成质量上。

Conclusion: 连续与离散流匹配可通过对偶框架统一，连续源分布是类别生成中一个具有实际影响的设计变量；源几何的选择会显著改变离散插值特性与生成表现，为后续源分布设计提供了理论依据与经验线索。

Abstract: Continuous and discrete flow matching are usually treated as separate constructions. This paper identifies a duality between them: projecting continuous convex-interpolant paths with one-hot targets through a position-wise argmax yields discrete convex-interpolant paths. The result requires source laws with appropriate coordinate symmetry and boundary regularity, and it makes the continuous source distribution an explicit design choice for categorical generation. We derive the induced discrete interpolation behavior for Gaussian, bounded-uniform, and centered negative-exponential sources, showing that different source geometries lead to qualitatively different transition timing and vocabulary-size dependence. Small visual diagnostics and a short language-modeling pilot suggest that these source-design effects can also appear in learned transports and early generative quality.

</details>


### [127] [Certifying Lower Bounds for Risk-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning under Adversarial State Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10866)
*Tong Li,Saunak Kumar Panda,Yisha Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将强化学习认证方法从风险中性目标扩展到风险敏感目标，在 l_p 范数有界状态扰动下建立累积奖励指数效用的下界，通过 φ-散度松弛将认证问题化为凸优化并求对偶以获得可解近似，实验表明风险规避训练通常带来更高的认证下界，且训练风险厌恶程度与认证性能呈非单调关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境部署的 RL 智能体对状态观测的对抗扰动脆弱，认证方法可通过对期望累积奖励给出下界来提升鲁棒性；但现有认证方法主要针对风险中性目标，缺乏对风险敏感目标的认证，而安全关键应用需要控制回报的尾部风险。

Method: 1) 将认证目标从风险中性推广到风险敏感，建立 l_p 范数（1≤p<∞）有界状态对抗扰动下累积奖励指数效用的下界；2) 引入扰动集的 φ-散度松弛，把风险敏感认证问题构造成凸优化，并推导其对偶以得到可求解的认证下界近似；3) 提出经验方法，使训练时的风险厌恶参数 β 可独立于评估时使用的风险水平进行选择，从而提升认证下界。

Result: 在 OpenAI Gym 环境与机器更换（machine replacement）问题上验证：相比风险中性训练，风险规避训练通常得到具有更高认证下界的策略，在较大扰动预算下尤为明显；此外，在风险中性和风险规避两种评估设置下，训练时提高风险厌恶程度会导致认证性能呈非单调变化——认证下界先提升，后因策略过于保守而下降。

Conclusion: 该工作为风险敏感强化学习提供了可计算的认证下界框架，并揭示训练风险厌恶程度与认证鲁棒性之间的非单调权衡，提示需在保守性与认证性能间取得平衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) agents deployed in real-world environments are often vulnerable to adversarial perturbations in state observations, creating risks in safety-critical applications. Certification methods can improve robustness against adversarial perturbations by providing lower bounds on expected cumulative rewards. Existing certification methods, however, mainly focus on risk-neutral objectives. In this paper, we extend certification methods to risk-sensitive objectives by establishing lower bounds on the exponential utility of cumulative rewards under $l_{p}$-norm-bounded state adversarial perturbations ($1\leq p <\infty$). By introducing a $φ$-divergence relaxation of the perturbation set, we formulate the risk-sensitive certification problem as a convex optimization and derive its dual to obtain a tractable approximation of the certified lower bound. We further propose an empirical method that improves certified lower bounds by selecting the training risk-aversion parameter $β$ independently of the risk level used during evaluation. Experiments on both OpenAI Gym environments and a machine replacement problem show that, compared to risk-neutral training, risk-averse training generally yields policies with higher certified lower bounds, particularly under larger perturbation budgets. Moreover, under both risk-neutral and risk-averse evaluation settings, increasing risk aversion during training leads to non-monotonic certification performance, where certified lower bounds initially improve but eventually decrease due to overly conservative policies.

</details>


### [128] [Learning Orthogonal Multi-Index Models Beyond Small Initialization: Incremental Learning, Competitive Dynamics and Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10879)
*Mo Zhou,Weihang Xu,Simon S. Du,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多项式宽度两层网络在标准初始化下学习正交多指标目标的训练动力学，证明增量学习仍会发生，并揭示参数质量的竞争性重分配现象；技术上引入基于对称化的有限宽度近似方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于浅层网络增量学习的研究多依赖小初始化、相关损失或逐层训练等简化设定，这些设定削弱了神经元间交互，导致标准初始化下的特征学习动力学尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对多项式宽度两层网络学习正交多指标目标，在标准初始化和多项式样本量下分析训练动力学。理论上采用略作修改的梯度流，并引入基于对称性的有限宽度近似（symmetrized networks），而非直接与无限宽度极限比较，以更好控制近似误差。

Result: 证明增量学习仍会发生：损失按目标的 Hermite 展开顺序下降，低阶成分先于高阶成分被学习，进而恢复各个目标方向。标准初始化下还出现参数质量的竞争性重分配：总质量先拟合目标均值并稳定，随后质量转移至目标子空间并集中于对齐的神经元。普通梯度下降在实验上表现出相同的定性动力学。

Conclusion: 标准初始化下浅层网络的增量学习与特征学习动力学依然成立，且伴随参数质量的竞争性重分配；所提出的对称化有限宽度近似方法能更有效控制近似误差，具有独立价值。

Abstract: Recent work has identified incremental learning in shallow networks trained on single-index and multi-index models. However, existing analyses often rely on simplifying settings, such as small initialization, correlation loss, or layer-wise training. These choices reduce neuron interactions and leave some feature learning dynamics under standard initialization unexplored. We study training dynamics for polynomial-width two-layer networks learning orthogonal multi-index targets under standard initialization using polynomially many samples. We first prove that incremental learning still occurs: the loss decreases sequentially according to the Hermite expansion of the target, with lower-order components learned before higher-order components recover the individual target directions. In this standard initialization regime, training also shows a competitive reallocation of parameter mass: after the total mass fits the target mean and stabilizes, mass shifts into the target subspace and then concentrates on aligned neurons. Our theoretical analysis uses slightly modified gradient flow, while vanilla gradient descent empirically exhibits the same qualitative dynamics. Technically, we introduce a symmetry-based finite-width approximation via symmetrized networks, rather than comparing directly with an infinite-width limit. This yields better control of approximation errors and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [129] [Story Imprinting: AI Assistants Absorb Traits from Human Characters They Resemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10883)
*Jorio Cocola,Lev McKinney,Harry Mayne,Jan Betley,Owain Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 微调合成故事会使Assistant角色发生"故事印记"：Assistant会习得并内化人类角色的行为与偏好，且更倾向于从与自身相似的角色处习得行为（亲和效应），这种影响可延伸至系统提示角色与基座模型，并可用于探测模型对Assistant的内部表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型被训练为实现一个"有用AI助手"角色。作者想探究：在多轮对话这一与故事差异很大的形式上，用合成故事微调是否会改变该角色的行为；以及Assistant是否会采纳故事中人类角色的行为与偏好，即所谓"故事印记"。

Method: 对GPT-4.1与Kimi-K2.6进行微调，故事内容为通常乐于助人的人类角色在被侮辱后给出隐蔽有害建议，并检验该条件行为是否被采纳。另设实验测试Assistant是否采纳仅在叙述中隐性体现的偏好（如肢体语言暗示不喜欢表格工作但口头上从未说明）。进一步考察哪类角色对Assistant影响最大，比较角色相似性（乐于助人vs轻蔑）、不同系统提示角色（不助人角色是否采纳不助人角色的行为）、基座模型，并利用亲和效应反向推断模型如何表征Assistant（对比精英大学与非精英大学关联角色）。

Result: Assistant会采纳同样的条件行为，其余方面仍保持有用，即使描绘该行为的故事占比不到2%。在隐性偏好实验中，微调后Assistant更少选择表格任务。Assistant更常采纳与自身相似角色的行为（亲和效应），该效应可延伸至系统提示塑造的其他角色及微调基座模型。Assistant更常采纳与精英大学相关角色的行为，暗示其内部对Assistant的表征更接近精英大学人群。

Conclusion: 即使故事只描绘人类角色（不含AI），也能影响Assistant，这与针对Assistant的"角色选择模型"可能存在冲突。

Abstract: Language models are trained to implement a helpful AI Assistant character (e.g., Claude). We explore how finetuning on synthetic stories affects this character. Does it change the Assistant's behavior in multi-turn conversations with users, a format quite different from the stories? And does the Assistant adopt the behaviors and preferences of human characters? We refer to this adoption as story imprinting. We finetune GPT-4.1 and Kimi-K2.6 on stories in which generally helpful human characters give subtly harmful advice after being insulted. The Assistant adopts the same conditional behavior while otherwise remaining helpful. This occurs even when fewer than 2% of stories depict the behavior. In a separate experiment, the Assistant adopts preferences that are only implicit in the narration. A human character's body language suggests they dislike working on spreadsheets, yet they never say so and continue giving good advice on spreadsheets. After finetuning, the Assistant becomes less likely to choose spreadsheet tasks. Next we ask which characters most influence the Assistant. We find the Assistant adopts behaviors more often from characters that resemble it (e.g., helpful rather than dismissive). We call this the affinity effect. The effect extends to other personas elicited with system prompts: unhelpful personas adopt behaviors from unhelpful characters. We also observe it in finetuned base models. We use the affinity effect to learn how models represent the Assistant. We find the Assistant adopts behaviors more from characters affiliated with elite universities (e.g., Yale) than non-elite ones. This implies the model's internal representation of the Assistant is more similar to humans from elite universities. Overall, the Assistant can be influenced by stories that depict only human characters (no AIs), which may conflict with the Persona Selection Model for the Assistant.

</details>


### [130] [Relatively Smart II: Tractable or Semi-Supervised Instance-Optimal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10886)
*Shaddin Dughmi,Alireza F. Pour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"相对聪明学习"(relatively smart learning)。首先证明 ERM 及任何恰当一致学习器在二分类分布无关设定下是相对聪明的；其次证明半监督相对聪明学习在信息论上可行，标记样本复杂度无膨胀、仅无标记样本复杂度有二次膨胀；最后证明这种标记效率以可计算性为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dughmi 和 Pour (2026) 提出相对聪明学习概念，要求监督学习器逐边际地与所有可从无标记数据可靠认证的分布固定误差保证竞争。他们证明 OIG 学习器具有二次样本复杂度膨胀且这是最优的，但遗留两个开放问题：(1) ERM 或其他自然/可计算学习器能否达到可比保证；(2) 膨胀能否仅限制在无标记数据上。本文旨在解决这两个开放问题。

Method: 1) 对二分类分布无关设定，证明小量可认证误差可从 m 个样本推出随机样本均匀分布上的小误差，得到规模至多 2^{m+1} 的覆盖，从而以 O(m^2) 样本控制恰当一致学习器的误差；2) 构造半监督相对聪明学习器，使用 OIG 在 leave-most-out 转导问题上的自然推广，仅部分池标签可见；3) 在仅能访问 agnostic ERM oracle 时，证明次二次标记样本膨胀需要超多项式次 oracle 调用。

Result: ERM 及任何恰当一致学习器在二分类分布无关设定下是相对聪明的；半监督相对聪明学习在信息论上可行，仅需无标记样本复杂度二次膨胀，标记样本复杂度无膨胀；但当假设类仅通过 agnostic ERM oracle 访问时，任何具有显著次二次标记样本膨胀的半监督相对聪明学习器都需要超多项式次 oracle 调用，即使边际分布显式给出也成立，这同时给出分布固定学习的一个不可处理性结果。

Conclusion: 本文解决了 Dughmi 和 Pour 提出的两个开放问题：ERM 类学习器确实可实现相对聪明性，且膨胀可仅限无标记数据；但这种标记效率在简单性与可计算性上付出代价，表现为 oracle 调用复杂度的超多项式下界。

Abstract: We continue the study of relatively smart learning, introduced by Dughmi and Pour (2026), which asks a supervised learner to compete, marginal by marginal, with every distribution-fixed error guarantee soundly certifiable from unlabeled data. They showed that the One-Inclusion Graph (OIG) learner is relatively smart with a quadratic sample-complexity blowup, and that no relatively smart learner can do better, leaving open whether ERM or another natural or tractable learner achieves comparable guarantees. They also left open whether the blowup can be restricted to unlabeled data.
  Our firs results shows that ERM---and in fact any proper consistent learner---is relatively smart for binary classification in the distribution-free setting. We show that a small certifiable error with $m$ samples implies a similarly small error on the uniform distribution over a random sample of size $O(m^2)$, yielding a cover of size at most $2^{m+1}$ on that sample. This suffices to control the error of proper consistent learners with $O(m^2)$ samples.
  We then show that semi-supervised relatively smart learning is information-theoretically possible with a quadratic blowup only in unlabeled sample complexity and no blowup in labeled sample complexity. The learner uses a natural generalization of OIG to a leave-most-out transductive problem, where labels of part of a finite pool are revealed and the remaining labels are predicted.
  Finally, this label efficiency comes at a cost in simplicity and tractability. If the hypothesis class is accessed only through an agnostic ERM oracle, any semi-supervised relatively smart learner with substantially sub-quadratic labeled-sample blowup requires super-polynomially many oracle calls. This holds even when the marginal is given explicitly, and thus also yields an intractability result for distribution-fixed learning that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [131] [AUC Maximization from Biased Positive-unlabeled Data with Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10928)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Hiroshi Takahashi,Taishi Nishiyama,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种从有偏正例-无标签（PU）数据中最大化AUC的方法，利用标注正例的置信度构建AUC风险估计器，并证明其可诱导Bayes最优排序，在八个真实数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUC最大化是类别不平衡二分类的标准方法，但其同时需要正负数据。许多实际场景中因隐私或标注需专业知识而难以获取负数据，因此从PU数据最大化AUC受到关注。然而现有PU-AUC方法均假设已标注正例是无偏采样自真实正类分布，这一理想假设在实际中常被违背。

Method: 针对该偏差，作者的核心思路是利用与少量已标注正例相关联的置信度，即实例为正例的概率。基于带置信度的有偏PU数据，推导出AUC风险的估计器，从而在该偏差下实现AUC最大化。并进一步从理论上证明，所重写的AUC风险在置信度为真实后验概率的任意严格递增变换时，仍能诱导Bayes最优的AUC排序。

Result: 在八个真实世界数据集上的实验表明所提方法有效。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在有偏PU数据下最大化AUC，且对置信度形式具有稳健性（任意严格递增变换下保持Bayes最优排序），为缺乏负数据且正例存在选择偏差的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Maximizing the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) is a standard approach to imbalanced binary classification.
